### [critical/both] claim_funding is unlatched: the poisoned funding index now pays $68,401.93 to 10 long holders, and the repo fix (R6) does not neutralise it

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/claim_funding.rs:99`  · prior: S3-01

**Evidence.** S3-01 predicted a $58,697–$72,322 long-side credit "latched until the crank resumes". The crank resumed today (faa6145): Market.mark_price_minute=56386 → 4 min old, twap_count=225, so mark_price_for_close() is Some and claim_funding.rs:61-63 no longer returns OracleStale. Market.cumulative_funding_index read live = -10_379_622_647_083_427_790 (-10.38, was -9.12 at audit). READ-ONLY simulateTransaction (sigVerify:false) of disc 0x07 against the LIVE program for every open long, post-state UserAccount.free_collateral at offset 40: EALRuxkJ…5v +$24,738.81 (4,997.53→29,736.33), Dfh5voM2…VW +$17,197.37, ENcorCq2…MJ +$13,627.31, 57y9nRtr…wV +$10,692.03, DLfMrAAk +$733.78, 3MibmeCz +$733.78, ESXD38Yk +$422.07, BjU1yihg +$422.07, GUfiKPym +$211.04, HrkyqEfy +$211.04; two positions pay (2Rwsf1Mw -$99.85, 88yuvr47 -$487.49). Net minted = $68,401.93 against $8,733 of total long notional (OI long 85.8 units × $101.79). Nothing debits a counterparty: deployed claim_funding.rs:99-104 does `free_collateral.checked_add((-payment) as u64)`; funding.rs:74-77 (deployed) multiplies notional by the unclamped 10.31e18 delta. $39,733.01 of it belongs to owners with pending_fills==0 and is withdrawable through withdraw_collateral now; $29,256.29 sits behind the S4-04 gate. Post-claim liabilities (users 619,666.89 + positions 32,459.39 + undelegated credits 13,813.24 + ER-live delegated credits 62,197.28 + legacy delegated 20,676.41 = 748,813.21) exceed the vault (725,915.85) by 22,897.36 USDC. `git diff 8914e31 HEAD -- claim_funding.rs` is empty and HEAD compute_funding.rs:49-51 only caps FUTURE increments; per remediate.md:510-513 "no already-accrued value is rewritten", so deploying R1–R7 leaves this $68.4k claimable.

**Failure.** Any of the 10 listed wallets signs one claim_funding (0x07) today and is credited up to $24,738.81 of USDC that nobody paid, then withdraws it from vault BTWVG…71qr; if all ten do so the vault is 22,897 USDC short and the last withdrawers of the 52 UserAccounts (551,264.96 USDC) are unpaid. The exposure survives the pending redeploy unchanged.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY, and R6 alone is not it: add an authority-gated one-shot that re-snapshots every live Position.funding_index_snapshot to the current index (or zeroes index+snapshots together) so the accrued -10.38 delta is forgiven before/at the R6 deploy; in the same release make claim_funding refuse when |index - snapshot| exceeds MAX_FUNDING_RATE_PER_INTERVAL × MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS × (intervals elapsed since snapshot). Until then the only mitigation that needs no key is none — the instruction is owner-signed and the mark is fresh.


### [high/deployed] S5-01/S5-X04 live: the deployed withdraw_trading_credit pays any ER-committed credit value; the entire 725,915.85 USDC vault is behind one validator key

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/withdraw_trading_credit.rs:53`  · prior: S5-01

**Evidence.** Deployed tree = 8914e31 (programdata 6BBrTYsX… last written slot 484488785 = 2026-08-16T15:38:54Z, six days before the first remediate commit d6d83bc 2026-08-22). Deployed withdraw_trading_credit.rs:53-64 checks only `credit.credit < amount` then `free_collateral.checked_add(amount)`; no `reserved_margin` cap (HEAD:63 `if user.reserved_margin < amount` absent). simulateTransaction of disc 0x28 (seed_credit_ledger) → InvalidInstructionData from the deployed `_ =>` arm (mod.rs:165 at 8914e31) while 0x27 reaches its handler (NotEnoughAccountKeys), proving R1's ledger instruction is not on chain. `git grep` at 8914e31 shows the only increment of `.credit` is fund_trading_credit.rs:71 (L1), so no unprivileged user can raise it — the exploiter is whoever authors committed bytes: every DelegationRecord PDA read (e.g. [b"delegation", dd4KqsiC…]) names validator authority MAS1Dt9qreoRMQ14YQuhg8UTZMMzDdKhmkZMECCzk57. Live census: 30 modern delegated credits (L1 snapshot 98,711.60 USDC, ER-live 62,197.28), 1 undelegated modern (500.00). Vault BTWVG…71qr = 725,915,845,954 atoms; withdraw_collateral (deployed :85-88) gates only `free_collateral < amount`. The 22 × 7.017e18 garbage credits the spec recorded on 2026-08-22 are gone from the live set (0 credits ≥1e12 on either endpoint today).

**Failure.** A hostile or compromised MAS1Dt… validator commits credit=725_915_845_954 on any delegated credit with committed=0, the owner undelegates and calls withdraw_trading_credit then withdraw_collateral, and the shared vault is emptied; the 52 honest UserAccounts (551,264.96 USDC free) can no longer withdraw.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY R1 (credit ledger cap at withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63-74 on HEAD) plus seed_credit_ledger for the 31 pre-existing funded credits; no repo change needed beyond what factory/remediate already carries.


### [high/deployed] S4-06/S4-07 live: deployed settle_from_log applies ER-authored filled_margin verbatim and never bounds count by capacity

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/settle_from_log.rs:108`  · prior: S4-06

**Evidence.** Deployed settle_from_log.rs:108-124 reads header.count/capacity and checks only `capacity == 0` and the byte length (`FillLogHeader::LEN + capacity * FillEvent::LEN > fl_data.len()`); with count > capacity the loop at :131-141 indexes `(head + processed) % capacity`, and the skip test `fill.sequence <= last_settled` compares against the PRE-loop snapshot, so each stored fill is applied count/capacity times (S4-07's 819× at count=65535). :226-246 passes `fill.filled_margin` to update_position for both legs, which does `pos.collateral.checked_add(fill_margin)` (settle_trades.rs:329-332 deployed) with no re-derivation (HEAD:132 `count > capacity`, HEAD:153 `sequence > u32::MAX`, HEAD:187 compute_initial_margin bound are all absent on chain). The 461 live FillLog PDAs (epochs 0–460, [b"fill_log",0u16,epoch u32]) are all owned by DELeGG… on L1, so only a commit from validator MAS1Dt… writes their bytes; the instruction itself is permissionless (no signer check at :44-60). update_position releases collateral to free_collateral on reduce/flatten (settle_trades.rs:363+ deployed), so minted collateral becomes withdrawable USDC. Also `market.rs:208` (deployed) writes `(seq as u32)`, so an ER-authored sequence ≥ 2^32 moves the cursor backwards for replay.

**Failure.** A validator-authored commit with count=65535 or filled_margin=725_915_845_954 on one fill lets a colluding maker/taker pair settle it once (anyone can call), flatten, and withdraw the vault; bounded only by the vault's 725,915.85 USDC.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY R4 (settle_from_log.rs and settle_trades.rs on HEAD already carry the four guards); nothing further in the repo.

### [high/deployed] S1-01 live: 4 legacy 56-byte credits (13,313.24 USDC) still unreachable, and the production UI offers a close path the deployed program rejects

`programs/slipstream/src/state/trading_credit.rs:53`  · prior: S1-01

**Evidence.** Deployed trading_credit.rs:53/68/86 (`data.len() < Self::LEN` with LEN=96) rejects every 56-byte account; deployed close_trading_credit.rs:54 loads through from_account_info so it inherits the rejection, and `read_common`/LEGACY_LEN (HEAD trading_credit.rs:54-77) do not exist on chain. READ-ONLY simulateTransaction of the exact 3-account close_trading_credit (0x1C) the shipped frontend sends (frontend/src/lib/slipstream/instructions.ts:631-650, called from use-session.ts:1280) for all four owners → InstructionError InvalidAccountData. session-panel.tsx:413-425 shows those owners "Close it to reclaim the rent, then start again" and the SDK comment (:603-608) promises the balance lands in free_collateral — true on HEAD (R2), false on the live program. A further 12 delegated 56-byte credits hold 36,470.00 USDC on L1 / 20,676.41 ER-live and are unreachable even after the redeploy (S5-02, one-way delegation).

**Failure.** BXDFvyrb… presses the button the live site shows and gets a failed transaction; 13,313.24 USDC across 4 wallets stays stranded until R2 is on chain, and 20,676.41 USDC (ER-live) across 12 more stays stranded after it.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY R2 (close_trading_credit legacy branch + refuse credit>0). Repo: until deployed, gate the LegacyNotice button in session-panel.tsx on a deployed-program capability probe (e.g. simulate the close first) so the UI does not advertise a path the live program cannot execute.


### [high/deployed] S2-01/S2-X01/S4-03/S4-01 live: all 9,544 fills since the audit are unsettled, 5,368 are already destroyed, 4,096 sit in a full ring, and anyone can jump the cursor

`programs/slipstream/src/state/order_book.rs:206`  · prior: S2-01

**Evidence.** Deployed order_book.rs:206-222 overwrites the oldest entry at capacity (HEAD :209 FillQueueFull absent); deployed mirror_fills.rs:124-152 skips `<= last_mirrored` and sets the cursor to the batch max with no gap check; deployed settle_from_log.rs:260-271 sets Market.last_settled_sequence = max_seq. Live ER OrderBook header (offset 0..48 on devnet.magicblock.app): head=tail=485, fill_event_count=4096=max, next_fill_sequence=53734 → oldest surviving 49638; L1 Market._padding2[0..4] = 44189 (44173 at audit: 16 fills settled in two weeks). Enumerating every FillEvent in all 461 committed FillLog PDAs (header is 24 bytes, not the 32 the fill_log.rs:107 comment claims): only 80 sequences > 44189 exist anywhere (48544–48623, mirrored identically into 30 epochs 430–459; epoch 460 count=0); ER copies of epochs 400–460 add nothing above 48623. So of 44190..53733: 80 recoverable (85.84 USDC filled_margin), 4,096 still in the ring but unmirrored (filled_margin Σ 4,261.06 USDC, prices 102,396–107,998 ×1e3, all 0.2-unit bot fills between makers 26ESj/Cq1Gw/4RHRj and takers biry5/Hjfon), 5,368 in neither (44190–48543, 48624–49637) = destroyed since the audit on top of the 33,146 it counted. The five bot credits read L1 20,009.45 vs ER-live 8,884.34 USDC (11,125.11 drained by fills) while all five bot Positions on L1 read size 0 / collateral 0. READ-ONLY simulateTransaction of settle_from_log(epoch 459, 80) with a compute budget and no signer of note: succeeds (344,342 CU), Market.last_settled_sequence → 48623, 26ESj Position → size 1.2 / collateral 6.41; the 4,354 sequences 44190–48543 are then past the cursor forever. fill-log keeper is stopped by design, so every new fill evicts one of the 4,096 unmirrored ones; a taker-fee of 6 bps on a $20.36 fill (~$0.012) makes evicting all 4,096 cost ≈$50 for any two wallets with a session.

**Failure.** Margin drained from TradingCredits in the ER (4,261.06 USDC in-ring + whatever the 5,368 destroyed fills carried, today bots' money, tomorrow any user's) never reaches Position.collateral on L1; restarting the deployed keeper on epoch 460 mirrors from 49638 and a single settle moves the cursor past the 80 recoverable fills too.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY R3+R4 together (lossless ring, gap-checked mirror, contiguous cursor). Runbook before that deploy: settle epoch 459's 80 fills FIRST (cursor → 48623), then accept that 44190–48543 and 48624–49637 are unrecoverable; do not restart the deployed fill-log keeper until R3 is live.

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/record_pending_fill.rs:61`  · prior: S10-01

Deployed record_pending_fill.rs:61, initialize_fill_log.rs:60, delegate_fill_log.rs:98 gate on `global.authority` only; simulateTransaction of disc 0x2A (set_keeper) → InvalidInstructionData, so R7's keeper role is not on chain and the key still needs to be hot wherever the fill-log keeper runs. A5sV4… holds 1.85 SOL.

### [low/deployed] Premise check: the deployed bytecode was built WITH overflow-checks; no arithmetic wraps on chain

`Cargo.toml:13`

**Evidence.** The workspace-root `[profile.release] overflow-checks = true` (Cargo.toml:13-14) landed in b5f512d on 2026-07-26, 21 days before the live upgrade (slot 484488785, 2026-08-16T15:38:54Z, tx 3UtrgCoq…). The 205,864-byte ELF dumped from programdata 6BBrTYsX… contains the strings "attempt to add with overflow", "attempt to subtract with overflow", "attempt to multiply with overflow", "attempt to negate with overflow" (1 each); a scratchpad build of HEAD with CARGO_PROFILE_RELEASE_OVERFLOW_CHECKS=false (180,312 bytes) contains none of them, while the repo's own target/deploy/slipstream.so (186,464 bytes) contains all of them. Those strings are emitted only by checked-arithmetic panics, so the live program aborts on overflow. The only narrowing conversion on ER-controlled input in the deployed tree is market.rs:208 `(seq as u32)` (cursor truncation, bounded on HEAD by settle_from_log.rs:153); `fill_qty as i64` at settle_trades.rs:317/319 is unreachable past compute_notional's checked_mul for qty ≥ 2^63.

**Failure.** No wrapping-arithmetic exploit exists against the deployed program; the brief's "overflow-checks OFF" premise would mis-prioritise remediation if carried forward.

**Fix.** None in code; correct the audit brief and the Cargo.toml:8-12 comment (which still describes the pre-July deploy) so future rounds do not re-derive it.


### [low/repo] deploy.json records deployedAt 2026-05-30 for a program last upgraded 2026-08-16, which is what produced the 'never redeployed' misread

`deploy.json:17`

**Evidence.** deploy.json:17 `deployedAt: 2026-05-30T09:00:00.000Z` while :18 lastUpgradeSignature 3UtrgCoq… confirmed on chain at slot 484488785 = 2026-08-16T15:38:54Z; e1304e1 (2026-08-16) changed only line 18. git log shows program-source commits on 2026-07-26/27 (b5f512d…8914e31) are in the live build and everything from d6d83bc (2026-08-22) onward is not; the twelve P0 fixes are indeed undeployed, but for a different reason than the field says.

**Failure.** Operators and auditors reading deploy.json date the live bytecode three months too early and misattribute which fixes (e.g. overflow-checks, oracle pinning, vault pinning) are live.

**Fix.** Update deployedAt alongside lastUpgradeSignature in the redeploy chore commit (one line); optionally record the program-source commit hash in the same file.

**Measured:** Deployed bytecode: program last upgraded on-chain at slot 484488785 = 2026-08-16T15:38:54Z (deploy.json lastUpgradeSignature 3UtrgCoq…); the 2026-08-22 remediate P0 fixes (incl. lossless fill ring, commit 385414d) are NOT deployed — diff e1304e1..HEAD confirms deployed push_fill_event is the overwrite ring. overflow-checks=true has been at the workspace root since 2026-07-26 (commit b5f512d), so it IS on in the deployed Aug-16 build; counter wraps abort rather than wrap silently (S2-C4 holds on deployed). Live ER order book 83zMFL…: fill_event_count=4096 (FULL), head==tail==485, next_fill_sequence=53734, max_order_slots=2048, active_order_count=30, free_slot_count=2018 (sum=2048, free-list+active invariant holds; L1 free list walked to 2037 entries, terminated clean). Ring holds sequences 49638..53733. Max last_mirrored_sequence over all 461 FillLog epochs (L1 and ER identical) = 48623; Market.last_settled_sequence = 44189. Destroyed-and-unmirrored fills = 49638 - 48624 = 1,014 (sequences 48624..49637). ER produced ~9,544 fills since the 2026-08-21 audit (44190->53734) while settlement advanced 16 (44173->44189). ER Clock runs ~6s ahead of L1 Clock and ~0s vs wall over 5 samples (independent sysvar). Live market 0: max_leverage=20, tick_size=1000, lot_size=1e8, taker_fee_bps=6, maker_rebate_bps=1, last_mark_price=101.786454, mark fresh (stamp age 2 min) — S2-05 divisions exact for these params. All 30 resting ER slots + 11 L1 slots carry expiry_ts==0, so S2-02 has no live victim. Quote vault BTWVG5…: 725,915.845954 USDC; aggregate free_collateral 551,264.962526 USDC across 52 UserAccounts (13 with pending_fills>0); 41 Position accounts. Credit population confirmed via on-curve PDA check: 22 delegated modern credits carry credit=7,017,392,213,830,636,841 with owner/market_index fields overwritten by ER garbage (market_index 25900, active_orders 59767) — the S5-01 hostile-ER minted values, verified identical on L1 and ER.

**Not covered:** Deep settlement-side accounting (S4/R1 reserved_margin ledger, settle_from_log/settle_trades value conservation) is out of this dimension's scope and left to prog-settlement — noted only where it gates the money impact of S2-01. The six ER-authored properties S2-C11 lists as assumed-not-verified under a hostile sequencer (price-level sortedness, intra-level FIFO, free-list integrity, index bounds, self-trade rejection, POST_ONLY cross) were re-confirmed sound for an honest program by live decode of the ER book but, as prior audit established, hold only if the sequencer runs the deployed bytecode; not re-filed. No transactions were constructed and no keys read — all devnet evidence is read-only getAccountInfo/getProgramAccounts against api.devnet.solana.com and devnet.magicblock.app.


### [high/deployed] Deployed ER fill ring is in the overwrite regime: 1,014 produced fills already destroyed with no on-chain signal (S2-01 live)

`programs/slipstream/src/state/order_book.rs:206`  · prior: S2-01

**Evidence.** Restates S2-01, now quantified live on the DEPLOYED bytecode. The program on chain was last upgraded at slot 484488785 (2026-08-16T15:38:54Z, sig 3UtrgCoq…); the lossless-ring fix (commit 385414d, 2026-08-22) is NOT deployed. The deployed push_fill_event (git e1304e1:order_book.rs:206-221) OVERWRITES the oldest entry at capacity and returns Ok — `self.header.fill_event_head = (self.header.fill_event_head + 1) % max` at :219, no drop counter. Live read of the ER book 83zMFL6cHjgXkQ7KRNcgtHaZ1fhyNgxhM8aMpPpEnMqe (devnet.magicblock.app): fill_event_count=4096 (FULL), head==tail==485, next_fill_sequence=53734 -> ring holds sequences 49638..53733. Max last_mirrored_sequence over all 461 FillLog epochs = 48623. oldest_surviving(49638) - (last_mirrored+1)(48624) = 1,014 sequences (48624..49637) were pushed to the ring, overwritten before the (stopped) mirror keeper took them, and exist in no FillLog. next_fill_sequence rose 44190->53734 (~9,544 fills) since the 2026-08-21 audit while Market.last_settled_sequence advanced only 44173->44189.

**Failure.** Each destroyed FillEvent strands its filled_margin: the maker's ER credit is debited on their next reconcile_credit with no matching Position.collateral ever created, and the taker's ER credit was already debited inside place_order. 1,014 fills are unrecoverable right now, and the ring is still full and still overwriting every new fill while the mirror keeper stays down. On mainnet the loss is real collateral.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY: redeploy HEAD, whose push_fill_event refuses with FillQueueFull instead of overwriting and whose mirror_fills drains the ring after mirroring. Repo already fixed; the on-chain bytecode is not. No repo change.


### [medium/both] Matching engine never reads expiry_ts; an expired resting order stays fillable (S2-02)

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/place_order.rs:420`  · prior: S2-02

**Evidence.** Restates S2-02, confirmed still real on HEAD (factory/remediate @ 87f9eb1) and on the deployed build. match_order's only maker snapshot is `(maker_slot.remaining_size, maker_slot.owner, maker_slot.side)` at place_order.rs:420-423 — expiry_ts is never consulted; the fill proceeds on price alone. expiry_ts is read only at cancel_order.rs:88 (permits a permissionless cancel, which requires someone to send a tx). Enforcement is entirely the expiry keeper, which is down. The remediate run touched place_order.rs (S2-X03 mark gate) but did NOT add an expiry check to the matcher. Deployed version identical (git e1304e1:place_order.rs:406-407 same 3-field snapshot).

**Failure.** A maker who used expiry_ts as a risk control is picked off at the stale price they explicitly stopped standing behind — the adverse-selection cost expiry exists to cap, unbounded in a gapping market. No live victim right now: all 30 resting ER slots and 11 L1 slots carry expiry_ts==0, so the exposure is to the next order placed with an expiry.

**Fix.** In match_order, before filling against a maker slot, skip and free it when `slot.expiry_ts > 0 && now >= slot.expiry_ts`, so enforcement does not depend on an off-chain scanner. NEEDS-DEPLOY once changed.


### [medium/both] Session-key expiry and permissionless cancel are decided by the ER-controlled Clock sysvar (S2-03)

`programs/slipstream/src/state/trading_credit.rs:138`  · prior: S2-03

**Evidence.** Restates S2-03, still real on HEAD and deployed. place_order.rs:146 and cancel_order.rs:50 take `now` from `Clock::get()` evaluated inside the ER; is_authorized_signer's session test is `now < self.session_expiry` (trading_credit.rs:143). cancel_order.rs:88 gates the permissionless expiry-cancel on the same clock. The sequencer supplies that sysvar. Measured: the ER Clock (devnet.magicblock.app) runs ~6s ahead of the L1 Clock and ~0s vs wall over 5 samples, i.e. the ER authors its own unix_timestamp independent of L1. A rewound ER clock revives every session key the ER has seen and lets any signer cancel every resting order carrying a non-zero expiry_ts.

**Failure.** On a hostile sequencer (mainnet), a session key the owner believes is dead is revived, binding the owner's margin after nominal expiry; and resting orders with an expiry can be force-cancelled. Bounded by one TradingCredit's credit per user per market. [mainnet-only]: devnet's ER is the trusted MagicBlock operator, so no adversary today.

**Fix.** Stamp session_expiry against an L1-anchored slot recorded at authorize_session, or require the owner's own signature for any action past nominal expiry, so ER-local time cannot terminate or revive a delegation. Program change -> NEEDS-DEPLOY.


### [low/both] orders_per_user gate narrows a u16 counter to u8 before comparing (S2-04)

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/place_order.rs:223`  · prior: S2-04

**Evidence.** Restates S2-04, still present on HEAD: `if (credit.active_orders as u8) >= orders_per_user`. active_orders is u16 (trading_credit.rs:33) and reconcile_credit (called two lines earlier at place_order.rs:210) overwrites it with a full book scan bounded only by max_order_slots (2048), not orders_per_user (20). The cast discards the high byte, so the cap is active_orders mod 256 — fires at 255 owned slots, not at 256. No invariant ties active_orders < 256 anywhere.

**Failure.** Not reachable on its own today (reaching 256 owned slots requires the gate already bypassed 256 times; live max is one owner holding 12 ER slots). A missing second line of defence: any future path that raises a user's slot count without this gate lets one account silently occupy the shared 2048-slot pool and lock others out with OrderBookFull.

**Fix.** Compare in the wider type: `if credit.active_orders >= orders_per_user as u16`. One line, no layout impact. NEEDS-DEPLOY.


### [low/both] place_order / cancel_order never PDA- or owner-pin the TradingCredit account (S2-X02)

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/place_order.rs:149`  · prior: S2-X02

**Evidence.** Restates S2-X02, still unfixed on HEAD. grep of both files shows every other account is pinned: global_state by owner+PDA (place_order.rs:72-78), market by owner+PDA (:112-122), order_book by PDA (:191-197, cancel_order.rs:60-66). trading_credit_acc is pinned by neither — TradingCredit::from_account_info (place_order.rs:149, cancel_order.rs:54) validates only length>=96 and disc byte==6, never `account.owner()==program_id` and never derives [SEED_CREDIT, credit.owner, market_index]. Authorization is 100% the is_authorized_signer field check plus the incidental fact that every path writes the credit and the runtime rejects a write to an account this program does not own. Under a HOSTILE ER (this dimension's premise) the ER authors the account bytes and chooses which account is passed, so the missing pin lets it substitute a fabricated disc-6 buffer with owner==attacker and credit==u64::MAX; but that is a subset of what S5-01/S2-03 already grant a hostile sequencer, so the incremental capability here is the honest-ER defence-in-depth gap only.

**Failure.** No live exploit demonstrated: under an honest ER the market_index binding (place_order.rs:153, cancel_order.rs:60-66) plus is_authorized_signer plus runtime write-protection force the passed account to be the caller's own canonical credit PDA. It is a defence-in-depth gap on the money-holding account of the two hottest instructions and the one place the file's own pinning discipline (place_order.rs:108-111) was not applied.

**Fix.** Derive [SEED_CREDIT, credit.owner, market_index] and compare to trading_credit_acc.key() (as delegate_trading_credit already does), plus an owner()==program_id check, in both instructions. NEEDS-DEPLOY.


### [low/both] taker_margin and filled_margin are computed by two formulas asserted equal only in comments (S2-05)

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/place_order.rs:441`  · prior: S2-05

**Evidence.** Restates S2-05, still real on HEAD. On the ER, the taker is debited compute_initial_margin(compute_notional(fill_qty, level_price), leverage) (place_order.rs:441-443), commented 'same-leverage MVP => taker_margin == filled_margin'. On L1, settle_trades credits the taker's Position.collateral with fill.filled_margin — the maker-derived floored proportional share from OrderSlot::drain_margin_for_fill (order_slot.rs:97-101). Nothing enforces the equality; it holds only when every floor is exact. Exact for the live market (measured: max_leverage=20, tick_size=1000, lot_size=1e8, so each per-lot notional is a multiple of 100 and 100/20=5), and initialize_market accepts any max_leverage in 1..=255.

**Failure.** Zero on the deployed market (divisions exact). On a market whose max_leverage does not divide the per-lot notional, each fill mints or destroys up to max_leverage-1 quote atoms of Position.collateral that no TradingCredit paid for, against the whole vault's backing. The load-bearing conservation invariant exists only as prose in two files that can drift.

**Fix.** Stamp the taker's own debit on the FillEvent (spare bytes exist) and have settlement credit each side what it actually paid; or assert taker_fill_margin == filled_margin on the ER side and fail the fill otherwise. NEEDS-DEPLOY.

### [high/both] withdraw_collateral identifies its UserAccount by discriminator + owner field, never by address; an ER-delegated 96-byte credit committed back with data[0]=3 is accepted as the UserAccount and pays the vault out under an ER-authored free_collateral, so R1's ledger never binds

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/withdraw_collateral.rs:69`  · prior: S5-X04

**Evidence.** withdraw_collateral.rs:69-73 gates the account with `user_account_acc.owner() != program_id`, `UserAccount::from_account_info` and `user.owner != *owner.key()`; grep of the file finds no SEED_USER derivation (mechanical map: every instruction file, `User=2/seedU=0`). `UserAccount::from_account_info` (state/user_account.rs:100-108) accepts any `data.len() >= 56` with `data[0] == 3`; `TradingCredit::LEN == 96` (state/trading_credit.rs:57, asserted by protected test_state.rs `test_trading_credit_size_and_idle`). delegate_trading_credit.rs:214-231 hands every byte of the credit PDA to the delegation program, and PRODUCT.md:43 records that the validator performs the ownership flip back — after which the credit PDA is a program-owned 96-byte account whose bytes, including data[0], were authored by the ER. docs/spec/remediate.md:157-213 (Decision 1) proves the ER cannot own the *UserAccount PDA's* bytes; nothing proves L1 reads only that PDA, and R1 moved the whole ceiling onto `reserved_margin` inside whatever account passes as a UserAccount (withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63). This is the identity gap S4-05 filed for the settlement finders ("a discriminator byte alone is not identity"), now on the one instruction that moves tokens out of the vault. Live (read-only, 2026-09-04T20:27Z): 52 delegation-owned 96-byte disc-6 credits exist, 22 of them already carrying an ER-authored `credit` of 7_017_392_213_830_636_841; vault BTWVG5oD… holds 725,915.845954 USDC. Deployed tree e1304e1 has the same lines (git diff e1304e1 HEAD on this file changes only the gate-2 comment).

**Failure.** Precondition: control of the ER validator — the exact S5-01 precondition R1 was built to close. Attack: (1) attacker A delegates an EMPTY credit PDA C (never funded, so A's real UserAccount keeps reserved_margin=0, pending_fills=0); (2) the ER commits C's 96 bytes as {disc=3, pending_fills=0, owner=A, free_collateral=725_915_845_954, reserved_margin=0} and finalizes undelegation, so C is now program-owned; (3) A sends withdraw_collateral(user_account=C, owner=A, quote_vault=BTWVG…, user_token=A's USDC ATA, vault_authority PDA, market=ECUp8…, global, positions=[SEED_POSITION,A,0]). Lines 69-73, 78, 85, 88 and 100-125 all pass, and lines 140-146 transfer the full vault balance to A. Every honest depositor's `withdraw_collateral` then fails. On the repo program the R1 ceiling (withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63) is never consulted; on the deployed program the same path exists alongside the unfixed S5-X04.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY. Add `UserAccount::load_pinned(acc, program_id)` that does the existing checks plus `find_program_address(&[SEED_USER, &user.owner], program_id).0 == *acc.key()`, and use it at every L1 UserAccount load (withdraw_collateral.rs:72, deposit_collateral.rs:54, fund_trading_credit.rs, withdraw_trading_credit.rs, close_user_account.rs:73, close_trading_credit.rs, close_position.rs:222, claim_funding.rs:78, liquidate_position.rs:111, seed_credit_ledger.rs, reset_pending_fills.rs, record_pending_fill.rs, settle_*). Three lines per site, one helper. This change is currently blocked by the protected tests — see the finding on test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs.

**Verifier verdicts (partial):**
- `?` → REFUTED: The exploit chain breaks at its step (2): no ER-authored TradingCredit bytes can ever come back under Slipstream ownership on either tree, so withdraw_collateral.rs:69 (`user_account_acc.owner() != program_id`) rejects the forged account and the vault-drain is unreachable now and with the repo program as-is.

Verified: the DLP's undelegate (magicblock-labs/delegation-program src/processor/fast/undelegate.rs, fetched read-only) has exactly two exits — (a) `if delegated_account.is_data_empty() { assign(owner_program) }` and (b) for any account with data: copy to an undelegate buffer, `close_pda(delegated_account)`, then `cpi_external_undelegate` into the OWNER program with `EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE_DISCRIMINATOR = [196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167]` (dlp-api/src/consts.rs:22) and then require `delegated_account data == buffer data` or fail with InvalidAccountDataAfterCPI. Slipstream never resizes the credit (delegate_trading_credit.rs:214-217 memsets 96 bytes, length stays 96), so path (b) is the only one; and Slipstream's dispatch has no handler for byte 196 — programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs (`_ => Err(ProgramError::InvalidInstructionData)`, same arm at e1304e1 mod.rs:165; `grep -rn "0xC4\|\[196" programs/slipstream/src/` is empty). The CPI fails, the undelegate tx aborts, and the account stays delegation-owned. The repo already knows this: keepers/src/provision-fresh-bots.ts:6-7 ("undelegate is a confirmed dead-end") and docs/03-ephemeral-rollups-and-delegation.md:94-95 ("No clean callback / stale ABI"); the claim's only support for the flip is the PRODUCT.md:43 prose. Live (read-only devnet, 2026-09-05): 52 delegation-owned 96-byte disc=6 credits (22 carrying 7_017_392_213_830_636_841), and exactly ONE Slipstream-owned 96-byte disc=6 credit (Bkcwr5R4jT2ovN7SyGzFnPQo2bTj7MLR3Ji7x9FsyUiY, credit=500 USDC) whose entire history is two Slipstream-only txs (slots 466201442/466201511) — it was never delegated. Zero credits have ever returned. The only DLP path that assigns back without the CPI is undelegate_with_rollback_after_timeout, which does `resize(0)` first and requires the owner program on the CPI stack (Slipstream never CPIs DLP), and a 0-byte account fails every `data.len() < LEN` load anyway. A malicious validator committing a 0-length state to hit exit (a) yields the same useless 0-byte account.

What survives is not new: "UserAccount is loaded by discriminator+owner with no SEED_USER derivation" at withdraw_collateral.rs:69-73 is the S1-03 secondary observation (docs/audit/audit-e2e/s1.md:233-239, explicitly graded "defence-in-depth only" because the owner field is written once from a PDA-deriving signer), and the type-confusion angle adds no reachable evidence. Not a restatement of a remediated P0 (R1 is orthogonal: it hardened the ceiling, not account identity), and not S5-X04 (that is withdraw_trading_credit's missing high-water mark). If re-filed it belongs at low/medium, target both, as "restates S1-03" with one genuinely forward-looking addendum worth recording against S5-02/K5: whenever the EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE handler is eventually added to make undelegation work at all, it must not copy the buffer verbatim — it must assert data[0]==DISC_TRADING_CREDIT and re-derive SEED_CREDIT — and L1 UserAccount loads should be SEED_USER-pinned before that hook lands, because at that moment this claim's chain becomes real.
- `?` → REFUTED: Guard exists upstream of withdraw_collateral: the attack's step (2) — "finalizes undelegation, so C is now program-owned" — is unreachable on both the deployed and the repo program, so withdraw_collateral.rs:69-71 (`user_account_acc.owner() != program_id` → IllegalOwner) always rejects an ER-authored credit.

Why the ownership flip cannot happen: (a) Solana's runtime lets a program change an account's owner only when the data is all-zero, so the delegation program (DLP) cannot hand a 96-byte credit with ER-authored bytes back to slipstream directly. (b) The DLP's only data-preserving return path is `process_undelegate` → `process_undelegation_with_cpi` → `cpi_external_undelegate`, which closes the account and CPIs the OWNER PROGRAM with data `[196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167] ++ borsh(seeds)` so the owner program re-creates the PDA and copies the buffer (delegation-program main, src/processor/fast/undelegate.rs, fetched to scratchpad/dlp_undelegate.rs lines 296-355, 394-433; the validator spec at ~/Documents/MB-evalidator/magicblock-validator/.agents/specs/validator-specification.md:293-299 says "Programs must include the corresponding instruction processor"). (c) Slipstream has no such processor: programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:113-177 does `split_first()` and matches the single byte 0x00..=0x2A with `_ => Err(ProgramError::InvalidInstructionData)`; `grep -rn "196, 28\|EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE\|process_undelegation" programs/` returns nothing. The CPI therefore fails and the whole DLP Undelegate tx reverts; the credit stays DLP-owned forever. (d) The DLP's only non-CPI return paths give back a 0-byte account (`is_data_empty()` fast path, undelegate.rs:221-241; `undelegate_with_rollback_after_timeout` does `resize(0)` then `assign`, and additionally requires slipstream itself on the CPI stack signing for the PDA, which no slipstream instruction does). A 0-byte program-owned account fails `UserAccount::from_account_info` (state/user_account.rs:44-46, len < 56), and the only writer of DISC_USER_ACCOUNT into an empty account is initialize_user.rs:57, which pins the PDA to [SEED_USER, owner] at :31-32 and CreateAccount's it at :48 (fails on an existing lamport-bearing account) — so the credit PDA address can never become a disc-3, >=56-byte, program-owned account.

Live proof on the DEPLOYED program (read-only, devnet): tx 5bPgmyYNYu3WE5zojVkPR8jREWvwengkXzpNAaJSVtH51NSA8Mb3QWexneD1RZg8enPreBZRbJ3a1wvogEnYCN7j, slot 479780451 (2026-07-28, after deployedAt 2026-05-30). The MagicBlock validator MAS1Dt9qreoRMQ14YQuhg8UTZMMzDdKhmkZMECCzk57 sent DLP Undelegate on credit 7n8WFubsCF2x5C223hEeujZYDodr7j5kJaCsFswcegSv (a slipstream credit — the CPI seeds decode to [b"credit", owner, u16 0]); inner CPI to 7qujfsb4… carried data bytes [196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167]…, logs read "Program 7quj… consumed 109 CU … failed: invalid instruction data" → "Program DELeGG… failed: invalid instruction data" → err InstructionError[3, InvalidInstructionData]. That credit is still DLP-owned today (it is one of the 52). Ten more of the 52 carry the same failed-undelegate tx pattern (bbUpyrn…, 8SJPP7…, 8cp8h4…, AHeeSS…, AnXbnA…, BUVh3b…, CPZWN5…, F3PAZP…, GuWT7z…). Exactly ONE program-owned 96-byte disc-6 account exists on devnet (Bkcwr5R4jT2ovN7SyGzFnPQo2bTj7MLR3Ji7x9FsyUiY) and its 2-tx history is initialize+fund, never delegated — i.e. no credit has ever come back from delegation. The repo tree (87f9eb1) has the identical dispatcher, so target "repo" is refuted on the same grounds.

Side notes for the caller: (1) the claim's "52 delegation-owned 96-byte disc-6 credits" is overcounted — the dataSize/disc filter also matches other programs' accounts (e.g. Dzbgf6ym… and G8tZsgTh… are BurnLpTokensRecept accounts of 2TrUiVhm…/ECctBoUD…). (2) The real, unfiled consequence of this evidence is that S5-02 is worse than written: undelegation of a slipstream credit is not merely un-schedulable from L1, it is impossible on both layers because the program lacks the DLP callback processor — every delegated credit (and the OrderBook/FillLog) is permanently DLP-owned. That is a funds-locked finding, not this vault-drain one. (3) The proposed PDA-pinning helper is still good hygiene (S4-05 identity principle) but is not needed to block this path.
- `?` → REFUTED (severity→low) (target→both): The L1 half of the claim is real but the ER half cannot land, so no exploitable path exists on either target.

L1 half (confirmed): withdraw_collateral.rs:69-73 gates the user account by program-owner + `UserAccount::from_account_info` (user_account.rs:42-51: len>=56, data[0]==3) + `user.owner == signer`, with no SEED_USER derivation; the repo's own protected test test_withdraw_collateral_regressions.rs:87 already passes a `Pubkey::new_unique()` user account through those gates to reach SameSlotWithdrawal (withdraw_collateral.rs:125). A 96-byte TradingCredit with data[0]=3 would parse as {pending_fills=market_index, free_collateral=credit, reserved_margin=committed}.

ER half (refuted): the only way ER-authored bytes can end up in a 7quj-owned account is the delegation program's `undelegate` (and `undelegate_confined_account`), which for any data-bearing account closes the account and CPIs the owner program with instruction data beginning EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE_DISCRIMINATOR = [196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167] (dlp-api/src/consts.rs:22; src/processor/fast/undelegate.rs `process_undelegation_with_cpi` / `cpi_external_undelegate`; identical in v1.0.0 src/processor/undelegate.rs:102-105,188,213,246), then asserts `delegated_account.data == undelegate_buffer.data` (InvalidAccountDataAfterCPI). SlipStream's dispatcher has no 0xC4 arm on the repo (instructions/mod.rs:117-177, `_ => Err(InvalidInstructionData)`) or the deployed tree (git show e1304e1 mod.rs:165), so the CPI reverts and the undelegate transaction fails: a delegated credit with data can never return to program ownership. The CPI-free paths return a 0-byte account (undelegate.rs `is_data_empty()` fast path; undelegate_with_rollback_after_timeout.rs:139 `resize(0)` + assign, which also requires SlipStream on the CPI stack and the program never CPIs the delegation program, per S5-02), and `UserAccount::from_account_info` rejects len<56 (user_account.rs:44). The disc-3 byte is otherwise written only by initialize_user.rs:58 behind the SEED_USER PDA check at initialize_user.rs:32-37 (`from_account_info_mut_or_init` for UserAccount has that single call site), so exactly one program-owned disc-3 account can exist per owner, and it is at the user PDA. This is the mechanism behind K5/S5-02 ("delegation is a one-way door"), which the claim overlooked.

Live confirmation (read-only devnet, 2026-09-05): the 52 UserAccounts all sit at their SEED_USER PDA; their 52 market-0 credit PDAs are 41 delegation-owned, 5 program-owned, 6 nonexistent; every one of the 5 program-owned credits has exactly 2 transactions and none touched DELeGGvX, i.e. no TradingCredit of this program has ever come back from delegation. The claim's "52 delegation-owned 96-byte disc-6 credits, 22 carrying 7_017_392_213_830_636_841" is a delegation-program-wide GPA that sweeps other programs' accounts; none of this program's 41 delegated credit PDAs carries that value (all credit fields are the funded amounts, max 20_000_000_000).

Residual (hygiene, not the claim): the missing PDA pin at withdraw_collateral.rs:69-73 restates S4-05's "discriminator alone is not identity" and would become live only if a 0xC4 external-undelegate handler that copies buffer bytes verbatim (the SDK reference does exactly that) is ever added to fix K5/S5-02 without also validating disc==6 and the SEED_CREDIT seeds. That is a low-severity note for whoever implements that handler, not an exploit reachable on the repo or the deployed program.


### [high/both] Position::LEN == TradingCredit::LEN (96): an ER-committed credit with data[0]=4 is a Position with arbitrary size/entry/collateral to close_position, claim_funding and liquidate_position, none of which pin the Position PDA — free_collateral is minted from a forged position and withdrawn

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/close_position.rs:104`  · prior: S4-05

**Evidence.** do_close pins the Market (close_position.rs:87-93) then loads the Position at :104 with no `owner()` check and no SEED_POSITION derivation, accepts it on `pos.owner == owner_key` (:106) and `pos.market_index == market.market_index`, and at :230 credits `settlement = collateral + pnl - funding` to free_collateral with `checked_add` (bounded only by u64). claim_funding.rs:44-46 and :102 do the same for a negative funding payment; liquidate_position.rs:107-112 accepts any Position whose owner equals the (also unpinned) UserAccount's owner. The mechanical map shows `Pos=2/seedP=0` for close_position, `Pos=3/seedP=0` for claim_funding, `Pos=2/seedP=0` for liquidate_position; the only Position readers that derive the PDA are withdraw_collateral.rs:113 and close_user_account.rs:92. tests/unit/src/test_security_regressions.rs:55-99 (protected) already names this exact 96-byte collision and blocks the PROGRAM-side write (authorize_session onto a Position) via the discriminator at state/trading_credit.rs:84 — but data[0] of a delegated credit is ER-authored (delegate_trading_credit.rs:214-231), so the discriminator is not a type check for those bytes. Cross-reference: S3-06 filed the same unbound-account pattern for cancel_trigger (still zero owner()/PDA checks, cancel_trigger.rs:30); S4-05 for settlement. Live: 41 program-owned 96-byte disc-4 Positions, 15 open, 32,459.39 USDC collateral; mark is fresh (last_mark 101.657197, circuit breaker 0) so do_close runs today.

**Failure.** Same precondition as the UserAccount variant (hostile ER). The ER commits credit PDA C as {disc=4, market_index=0, owner=A, size=+1, entry_price=1, collateral=X, funding_snapshot=current index} and finalizes undelegation. A calls close_position(market=ECUp8…, position=C, user_account=A's real UserAccount, owner=A): all gates pass, `settlement ≈ X + (mark-1)` is added to A's free_collateral at :230. withdraw_collateral's same-slot loop (:113-125) pins A's REAL Position PDA, which is untouched, so the withdrawal goes through if A never funded a credit (reserved_margin=0). Vault-bounded (725,915 USDC). claim_funding with a forged huge |size| and stale snapshot pays `-payment` at :102 with no cap; liquidate_position lets any liquidator route a forged position's settlement into the victim-named UserAccount.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY. `Position::load_pinned(acc, program_id)`: existing checks plus `find_program_address(&[SEED_POSITION, &pos.owner, &pos.market_index.to_le_bytes()]) == acc.key()`; call it in do_close (:104), claim_funding.rs:44, liquidate_position.rs:107, place_trigger.rs, execute_trigger.rs and both settlement finders. Blocked by protected fixtures at test_close_position.rs:110-112 and test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:68-69 (Pubkey::new_unique()).

**Verifier verdicts (partial):**
- `?` → stands (severity→high) (target→both): Not a restatement. Verified at HEAD 87f9eb1: close_position.rs:104 (do_close), claim_funding.rs:44 and liquidate_position.rs:107 load the Position via Position::from_account_info (state/position.rs: only len>=96 and data[0]==DISC_POSITION), with no owner() check and no SEED_POSITION derivation; grep of find_program_address/owner() across those three files hits only the Market/intent pins. Against the 12 remediated P0s: R1 bounds only withdraw_trading_credit by UserAccount.reserved_margin (withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63) and R4 bounds only settlement's filled_margin; neither touches these Position readers, and close_position.rs:222-231 adds settlement to free_collateral without consulting reserved_margin, so an ER-committed credit PDA with data[0]=4 bypasses every TradingCredit reader R1 hardened. This falsifies docs/spec/remediate.md Decision 3's 'the ER cannot cause a single atom more to leave the vault than the user put in'. Against prior findings: S1-C3 explicitly CLEARED Position/TradingCredit type confusion on the write-path disc check — the new evidence is that the disc byte of a delegated credit is ER-authored (S5-01 already established the ER owns every byte; withdraw_collateral's Position pin at :113-125 checks only the REAL PDA, which is untouched), so that clearance does not hold. S4-05 is the same unpinned-Position pattern but scoped to settlement finders and rated P2 on the runtime-write-protection argument, which does not apply here because the undelegated credit returns program-owned (live: 1 program-owned 96-byte disc=6 credit, 41 disc=4 Positions — matches the claim's count). Not one of the 2026-09-04 established items. Target 'both' is correct (no PDA pin at HEAD; close_position.rs last touched 2026-07-27 and never gained one, so the 2026-05-30 deploy lacks it too). Severity high is consistent with the rubric: exploit needs the single hostile-ER precondition, the same precondition the prior audit graded P0 [mainnet-only]. Fixtures at test_close_position.rs:110 and test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:68 use Pubkey::new_unique() for the position, so the PDA-pin fix would break protected tests — correctly flagged as NEEDS-DEPLOY and a finding rather than a fix.
- `?` → REFUTED (severity→low) (target→both): The failure path cannot be constructed, even granting the hostile-ER precondition. The 96-byte layout collision and the missing SEED_POSITION derivation at close_position.rs:104-117, claim_funding.rs:44-57 and liquidate_position.rs:107-112 are real, but the chain breaks one step earlier: ER-authored bytes can never land in a slipstream-OWNED account.

1. The claim's step "finalizes undelegation" is impossible. The MagicBlock delegation program's L1 `undelegate` (fetched: magicblock-labs/delegation-program main src/processor/fast/undelegate.rs:224-285, identical design in v1.0.0 src/processor/undelegate.rs:103-213) only assigns the account back directly when its data is EMPTY; for a non-empty account it closes it, then CPIs the owner program with EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE_DISCRIMINATOR = [196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167] (consts.rs:11) and requires the owner program to re-create the PDA and copy the buffer, then asserts data == buffer (InvalidAccountDataAfterCPI). Slipstream's dispatcher (programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:116-121) `split_first()`s a single u8 and matches only 0x00..0x2A; byte 196 (0xC4) hits `_ => Err(ProgramError::InvalidInstructionData)` at mod.rs:177 on this branch (master: mod.rs:165). `git log --all -S"EXTERNAL"`/`-S"196"` over programs/ is empty: no version, including the 2026-05-30 deployed one, ever had that arm. So every undelegate of a data-bearing TradingCredit reverts inside the delegation program, and the credit stays owned by DELeGG… forever (this is a stronger form of S5-02, not a forgery vector).

2. While the credit is DELeGG-owned, none of the three sinks can complete. Each one WRITES the position: close_position.rs:193-211 (size/entry/collateral zeroed or reduced, realized_pnl), claim_funding.rs:74-75 (funding snapshot advanced; payment != 0 requires the snapshot to change) and liquidate_position.rs:211-220 (size=0 etc.). Pinocchio's borrow_mut_data_unchecked skips the owner check but the runtime does not: any changed bytes in an account not owned by the executing program fail the instruction with ExternalAccountDataModified, and the free_collateral credit at close_position.rs:228-231 / claim_funding.rs:100-103 rolls back with it. The only read-only Position loads are withdraw_collateral.rs:124 and close_user_account.rs:103 (both PDA-pinned) and place_trigger.rs:79 (moves no money).

3. Live devnet (public RPC, 2026-09-05): getProgramAccounts(7qujfsb4…, dataSize=96) -> 42 accounts, disc {4:41, 6:1}; getProgramAccounts(DELeGG…, dataSize=96, disc=6) -> 52 delegated credits. The single program-owned 96-byte credit Bkcwr5R4jT2ovN7SyGzFnPQo2bTj7MLR3Ji7x9FsyUiY has exactly 2 lifetime signatures (slots 466201442/466201511, 26s apart, 2026-05-31) so it was never delegated. Zero credits have ever returned from delegation, consistent with (1).

4. Independently, the precondition is privileged, not unprivileged: fast/undelegate.rs:135-139 and :165-169 require the signer to hold an admin-initialised validator fees vault and to match delegation_record.authority, i.e. a MagicBlock-whitelisted validator key. That is the same trust root that already custodies every delegated credit's `credit` field (S1-C8 / S5-X04); the disc-collision adds no capability to it.

The unbound-Position-PDA pattern itself (S3-06 / S4-05 lineage) remains a defensible hygiene finding for do_close/claim_funding/liquidate_position, but the stated failure (free_collateral minted from a forged credit-as-Position, vault-bounded) has no reachable trigger on either the repo or the deployed program.
- `?` → REFUTED: REFUTED — a guard elsewhere makes the precondition unreachable: ER-authored TradingCredit bytes can never become Slipstream-program-owned, and every money-moving Position consumer writes to the Position, so a DLP-owned forgery is rejected by the runtime.

1. The forged credit cannot be undelegated back to the program. MagicBlock's delegation program (`src/processor/fast/undelegate.rs`, `cpi_external_undelegate`) re-creates a non-empty delegated account by CPI-ing the OWNER program with the 8-byte `EXTERNAL_UNDELEGATE_DISCRIMINATOR` `[196,28,41,206,48,37,51,167]` (dlp-api/src/consts.rs:22) and then requires the data to equal the buffer. Slipstream's dispatcher at programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:113-177 `split_first()`s a 1-byte discriminator and matches only 0x00–0x2A; byte 196 hits `_ => Err(ProgramError::InvalidInstructionData)` (mod.rs:177). Same on the deployed bytecode and on factory/remediate. So dlp's `undelegate` always fails for a Slipstream credit with data; the account stays owned by DELeGG… forever. `delegate_trading_credit.rs:214-231` only hands the bytes to the ER — nothing ever hands them back.

2. Live proof (devnet, 2026-09-05, slot 493386865): 30 Slipstream TradingCredits are DLP-owned (delegation-record owner == 7qujfsb4…, authority MAS1Dt9q… for all 30); 12 have `undelegation_requester=Validator, last_commit_id=1` and are stuck. Every validator-signed undelegate tx on them fails identically — e.g. 3HvYi5VRLt15dL7yvkheZAopfPzVXwYoVicL9UnE1rgAE9ia97sWmr1W9Gpvsh9cxva2We3XJKPMKwVk2K3dSMpq (slot 479066623): `Program 7qujfsb4… consumed 109 CU … failed: invalid instruction data` → `Program DELeGG… failed`. Same log on 7n8WFu… (slot 479780451), 8cp8h4… (490803909), GuWT7z… (490803472), CPZWN5… (479066227). The only program-owned 96-byte credit, Bkcwr5R4jT2ovN7SyGzFnPQo2bTj7MLR3Ji7x9FsyUiY, has 2 txs, both Slipstream-only — never delegated. No Slipstream credit has ever completed undelegation.

3. While DLP-owned, the forgery cannot move money: close_position.rs:193-219 always changes `size` (full close sets 0; partial subtracts ≥1 lot); liquidate_position.rs:211-221 zeroes size/entry/collateral; claim_funding.rs:74-75 rewrites the funding snapshot, and math/funding.rs:72-78 gives payment==0 exactly when snapshot==index, so any nonzero payment implies a byte change. A data change on an account the program does not own fails the transaction (ExternalAccountDataModified / read-only direct mapping). settle_trades.rs:348 loads mut as well. place_trigger.rs:79 is read-only but moves no money; execute_trigger.rs:63 routes into do_close.

4. The only dlp path that skips the CPI is the `is_data_empty()` fast path (finalize.rs:176 can resize to 0). That yields a 0-byte program-owned account, which no Position reader accepts (position.rs:39 requires len ≥ 96) and which only initialize_position.rs:55-61 could re-type — pinned to `[SEED_POSITION, owner, market_idx]`, so a credit PDA fails InvalidPda. `undelegate_with_rollback_after_timeout` needs the owner program to CPI with the delegated account as signer — no such Slipstream instruction exists, and it applies no ER commit anyway.

Notes for the orchestrator (not this claim): (a) the guard is accidental — the missing external-undelegate handler is itself the reason every delegated credit is stranded (30 accounts today; overlaps S5-02/S1-X04, which blamed the magic-program discriminator; undelegate_trading_credit.rs:36-40's "the TradingCredit is owned by the program again" is false on chain). (b) The referenced "UserAccount variant" shares this precondition — UserAccount is never delegated (trading_credit.rs:8) — so it should be re-checked against the same guard. (c) The missing Position PDA pin at close_position.rs:104 / claim_funding.rs:44 / liquidate_position.rs:107 remains a P2-grade hygiene gap (restates S3-06/S4-05 pattern), but with the discriminator check at position.rs:42/61 and no writer able to put disc=4 into program-owned non-Position bytes, it is not exploitable now.

### [medium/repo] The protected regression suite addresses UserAccount / TradingCredit / Position at Pubkey::new_unique(), so the PDA pin that closes the two forged-account paths cannot land without editing frozen tests — the harness freezes the identity gap in

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/tests/unit/src/test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:80`

**Evidence.** Protected files that construct program state at random addresses: test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:80-81 and :138-139 (`user_pk`, `credit_pk` = Pubkey::new_unique(), passed to withdraw_trading_credit / seed_credit_ledger); test_legacy_credit_recovery.rs:71-72 and :138-139 (close_trading_credit legacy path with a random user_pk and credit_pk); test_pending_fills_regressions.rs:55 (user_pk random into reset_pending_fills); test_cancel_order_expiry_regressions.rs:114,152,188,223 (trading_credit_pk random, 4 tests); test_close_position.rs:110-112 (position, user, owner all random); test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:68-69,195-196; test_settle_trades_regressions.rs:164-165,254-255. Only test_close_user_account_regressions.rs:43 derives `[SEED_USER, owner]`. The frozen check docs/checks/remediate/R1.md:58 grades `LEN == 96/56` only, never address binding. Per the run rules ("any fix that would require changing these is NOT a fix"), a `find_program_address` pin in withdraw_trading_credit, close_trading_credit, reset_pending_fills, cancel_order, do_close, liquidate_position or settle_* fails at least 13 protected tests.

**Failure.** The two high findings above have a three-line fix that the protected-test rule forbids; every future slice that tries to pin identity on the money path will be refused for the same reason, so the gap persists by construction rather than by oversight.

**Fix.** Policy, not code: explicitly unfreeze the address fixtures (two lines per test: `Pubkey::new_unique()` → `Pubkey::find_program_address(&[SEED_USER, owner.as_ref()], &program_id)` as test_close_user_account_regressions.rs:43 already does) in the same change that lands the pins, and add an address-binding assertion to the R1 frozen check so LEN and identity are graded together.


### [medium/both] deposit_collateral, withdraw_collateral, crank_twap and compute_funding still read a Market without pinning [SEED_MARKET, market_index]; S1-03's 'a forged Market is not constructible' holds only for L1 callers — a delegated OrderBook (≥2064 bytes) committed with data[0]=2 is one

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/deposit_collateral.rs:65`  · prior: S1-03

**Evidence.** Restates S1-03 / S1-X01 / S1-X02 with the coverage map re-run on 87f9eb1: SEED_MARKET count is 0 in deposit_collateral.rs (Market pinned only by owner()+disc at :65-72), withdraw_collateral.rs (:51-58), crank_twap.rs and compute_funding.rs; place_order.rs:112-122, close_position.rs:87-93, claim_funding, liquidate_position, settle_*, set_market_oracle pin it. New: execute_trigger's half of S1-X02 is effectively closed — it reads the unpinned Market at execute_trigger.rs:74-84 only for `market_index` and `is_met`, then do_close re-derives the PDA from the same account before any state change, so a forged Market cannot pass. New constructibility evidence: `Market::from_account_info` (state/market.rs:70-78) needs only `len >= 2064` and `data[0]==2`; the OrderBook PDA (612 KB) and every FillLog are delegated (delegate_orderbook.rs:189, delegate_fill_log.rs:195), so under the S5-01 precondition their committed bytes can carry disc=2 and any `quote_vault`. Live: exactly 1 disc-2 account (market_count=1), market.quote_vault = BTWVG5oD… (correct).

**Failure.** Hostile ER commits the OrderBook as a Market whose quote_vault is an attacker-owned token account of a worthless mint and finalizes undelegation; deposit_collateral(market=OrderBook) at :65-72 pins the destination to that account, :75-86 transfers worthless tokens and credits free_collateral 1:1; withdraw_collateral against the real Market pays real USDC. Weaker than the UserAccount forgery above (needs the book undelegated, which halts matching) but a distinct fix site. Without a hostile ER the exposure remains S1-03's second-market case (a user withdraws market B's vault against market A deposits).

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY. Copy the 8-line pin from place_order.rs:112-122 into deposit_collateral.rs after :65, withdraw_collateral.rs after :51, crank_twap.rs after :27 and compute_funding.rs after :23. Protected tests already pass PDA-derived markets (test_withdraw_collateral_regressions.rs:153 `test_withdraw_rejects_vault_not_matching_market`), so this one is landable.


### [medium/both] withdraw_collateral never requires user_token_acc != quote_vault_acc; a vault-to-vault self-transfer signed by the vault_authority PDA moves nothing while free_collateral is debited (S1-06 still open, still untested)

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/withdraw_collateral.rs:140`  · prior: S1-06

**Evidence.** withdraw_collateral.rs:140-146 builds `Transfer{from: quote_vault_acc, to: user_token_acc, authority: vault_authority_acc}` with no comparison of the two keys anywhere in the file; :148-152 debits free_collateral unconditionally. `user_token_acc` has no owner, mint or distinctness constraint (the only constraint on it is SPL's own). No test in tests/unit/src exercises the aliased case (grep for self-transfer / same-account withdraw returns nothing). Not CONFIRMED by execution here: the spl-token processor source is not in the local cargo registry (only pinocchio-token, which builds the CPI), so the upstream early-return could not be cited by line; S1-06's settlement criteria still stand.

**Failure.** Self-inflicted only: the caller burns their own free_collateral into an unclaimable vault surplus; the vault-balance = Σ free_collateral + Σ credit + Σ position collateral identity (live surplus today 29,666.66 USDC excluding the 22 impossible credits) drifts silently, and no third party can trigger it.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY. One line before :140: `if user_token_acc.key() == quote_vault_acc.key() { return Err(SlipstreamError::InvalidVault.into()); }`. Landable: no protected test passes the vault as the destination.


### [low/both] session_expiry is still unbounded in authorize_session and delegate_trading_credit, and docs/06-session-keys.md still calls it a hard wall — live measurement: no active session on any of the 52 delegated credits, so nothing is abusing it today

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/authorize_session.rs:54`  · prior: S1-07

**Evidence.** authorize_session.rs:54 parses the i64 and :61 stores it; no Clock, no ceiling. delegate_trading_credit.rs:177-184 stores it the same way and, when `data.len() < 40`, deliberately leaves any earlier session in place across a re-delegation (:41-43). The only bound is client-side: frontend/src/hooks/use-session.ts:62 `SESSION_TTL_SECS = 24h`. docs/06-session-keys.md:69 and :100 still present expiry as program-enforced. Live (2026-09-04T20:27Z): across 52 delegated credits both the L1 committed copy and the ER live copy show 0 active sessions, 0 expiries beyond 7 days, max stored expiry 1787925475 (2026-08-28T13:57:55Z); the 1 program-owned credit has expiry 0. Related: S2-03 (ER-controlled clock decides expiry) means even a bounded value is enforced by the ER's clock, not L1's.

**Failure.** A non-shipped client (or a modified one) mints a permanent trading co-signer on a credit; the docs' leaked-key blast-radius table row 'Act after expiry: No' is a client policy, not an on-chain property. No funds move (session keys reach only place/cancel), which is why it stays low.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY (or a docs fix). In authorize_session.rs:54 and delegate_trading_credit.rs:180: `let now = Clock::get()?.unix_timestamp; if session_expiry > now + MAX_SESSION_TTL_SECS { return Err(InvalidExpiryTimestamp) }` with a 7-day constant. test_security_regressions.rs:78 passes i64::MAX but already expects rejection, so it stays green.


### [low/repo] initialize_trading_credit and fund_trading_credit never check market_index against GlobalState.market_count, and the R1 ledger is one number per user across all credits — a credit for a nonexistent market can be created, funded and delegated, and seed_credit_ledger's single-credit overwrite then mis-sizes the ceiling for the others

`/home/anshtyagi/Documents/slip-grant/SlipStream/programs/slipstream/src/instructions/initialize_trading_credit.rs:36`

**Evidence.** initialize_trading_credit.rs:36 takes any u16 and :67 stamps it; the instruction takes no GlobalState account at all. fund_trading_credit.rs pins GlobalState (:34) for the pause flag but never reads market_count; delegate_trading_credit derives seeds from the stored index and delegates it. `UserAccount.reserved_margin` (state/user_account.rs:73-94) is per-user, raised by every fund (fund_trading_credit.rs:75-78) and SET, not added, by seed_credit_ledger.rs:79-82 from ONE credit (`credit.credit.min(amount)`). place_order on such a credit fails (no OrderBook PDA for that index), so no matching exposure.

**Failure.** A user with credits on index 0 and index 9 (or, later, on two real markets) whose ledger the operator repairs via seed_credit_ledger against one credit gets `reserved_margin` = that credit's balance only; withdraw_trading_credit on the other then fails CreditCeilingExceeded (withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63) until the authority re-seeds. Operator friction and a wrong ceiling, not a loss.

**Fix.** NEEDS-DEPLOY. In initialize_trading_credit take GlobalState and reject `market_index >= global.market_count` (mirrors initialize_market's pin at :214-224); document that seed_credit_ledger must be called with the SUM across the user's credits, or make it additive on the first seed only.
