**Measured:** Live devnet (2026-09-05, api.devnet.solana.com + devnet.magicblock.app, reads only): GlobalState len 104, market_count 1, paused 0, authority A5sV4PkkVM4gm3rejACvKFgxEMmj8ouGsffSKT5qYVc8, no pending_authority/keeper extension. Market len 2064, markPriceMinute 56407 (age 3 min at now_min 56410), lastMark 101.774227, restricted 0, lastSettledSequence 44189, twapCount 225. ProgramData 6BBrTYsX6ioS9Fu9uAPiLPRVDsBxCvy6wYWtzi85WihN last_deploy_slot 484488785 = 2026-08-16T15:38:54Z (deploy.json:17 says 2026-05-30; remediate commits are 2026-08-22+, so live bytecode predates all 12 P0 fixes but postdates 0x25-0x27). Read-only simulateTransaction arm probe: 0x24,0x25,0x26,0x27 -> NotEnoughAccountKeys (arm exists on chain); 0x28,0x29,0x2A,0x2B -> InvalidInstructionData (absent on chain). TradingCredit (disc 6) on L1: program-owned 56B = 4 accts, 13,313.24 USDC; program-owned 96B = 1 acct, 500.00 USDC; delegation-owned 56B = 12 accts, 36,470.00 USDC; delegation-owned 96B = 52 accts (L1 shadow sums to an absurd 154,382,628,802,985.63 USDC = stale/ER-authored bytes, S5-01 territory). ER TradingCredit: 37 accts (16 at 56B, 21 at 96B), 96,475.33 USDC total. UserAccount (disc 3): 52 accts, 0 with credit ledger>0, 13 with pending_fills>0, free_collateral total 551,264.96 USDC. Error tables: error.rs 59 names; frontend errors.ts 56; keepers ertx.ts 56; crank_twap_harness.ts 53; liquidation.test.ts 53; client/src none. SDK diff sizes: accounts 58+/47-, instructions 18+/190-, constants 19+/12-, pda 10+/19-. Node check: Buffer.alloc(56).readBigInt64LE(88) -> RangeError ERR_OUT_OF_RANGE.

**Not covered:** keepers/.bot-keys is absent locally, so I could not determine whether any bot wallet sits on one of the 16 legacy 56-byte credits (which would make finding 3 an active guard bypass rather than a latent one). Did not simulate full instructions against the deployed bytecode beyond the discriminator probe, so the account-order parity of the 37 shared builders is verified against repo source only (the Aug-16 deploy could differ from HEAD in any handler destructure; nothing in git history suggests it does). ### [high/repo] Neither SDK copy can build the three remediate instructions (0x28 seed_credit_ledger, 0x29 reset_pending_fills, 0x2A set_keeper), so the P0 fixes have no operational handle the moment they are deployed

`client/src/constants.ts:94`  · prior: S6-03

**Evidence.** programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:101-110 declares IX_SET_MARKET_ORACLE=0x25 .. IX_SET_KEEPER=0x2A (46 arms, 0x00-0x2A). client/src/constants.ts:94 and frontend/src/lib/slipstream/constants.ts:99 both stop at IX_EXECUTE_TRIGGER=0x24. grep for 0x28/0x29/0x2A/SEED_CREDIT_LEDGER/RESET_PENDING_FILLS/SET_KEEPER across *.ts/*.mjs: only hit is keepers/src/set-market-oracle.ts:18 hand-rolling 0x25. docs/spec/remediate.md:566-586 (R1 file set), :660-668 (R5), :696-702 (R7) list no TS builder. Read-only simulateTransaction against the deployed program: disc 0x24-0x27 -> NotEnoughAccountKeys (arm exists), 0x28/0x29/0x2A -> InvalidInstructionData (arm absent), so the live bytecode is the Aug-16 build and these arms only exist in repo. Live L1 state the runbook must repair with those instructions: 52 UserAccounts, 0 with credit ledger (offset 48) > 0, 13 with pending_fills > 0; funded credits = 4 program-owned 56-byte (13,313.24 USDC) + 1 program-owned 96-byte (500.00) + 12 delegation-owned 56-byte (36,470.00) + 52 delegation-owned 96-byte. Handler shapes with no TS mirror: seed_credit_ledger.rs:37 [global_state, user_account, trading_credit, authority] + u64 amount; reset_pending_fills.rs:45 exact 3-account destructure [global_state, user, authority] (no `..`, extra accounts revert); set_keeper.rs:43 [global_state, authority, system_program] + 32-byte key. Extends S6-03 (which counted 3 missing arms; now 6).

**Failure.** On deploy of this branch: withdraw_trading_credit.rs:63 refuses every grandfathered credit with CreditCeilingExceeded until seed_credit_ledger runs (46 credits, >=50,283 USDC visible on L1 plus the ER-side balances); withdraw_collateral stays refused for the 13 accounts with pending_fills>0 until reset_pending_fills runs; record_pending_fill/initialize_fill_log/delegate_fill_log fall back to authority-only until set_keeper runs. None of the three can be invoked from client/src, frontend, keepers, or tests without hand-rolling bytes, which is the pattern S6-03 already flagged as the source of wrong offsets.

**Fix.** Add IX_SET_MARKET_ORACLE..IX_SET_KEEPER (0x25-0x2A) to client/src/constants.ts and six builders to client/src/instructions.ts mirroring the destructures above (seed_credit_ledger: 4 keys + Buffer.alloc(9) u64 amount; reset_pending_fills: exactly 3 keys, 1-byte data; set_keeper: 3 keys + 33 bytes), then re-vendor. No program change; no deploy.


### [medium/repo] Four hand-copied SlipstreamError name tables (56/56/53/53 names) lag error.rs (59); the browser will render the new CreditCeilingExceeded as 'custom 0x138' on its own withdraw path

`frontend/src/lib/slipstream/errors.ts:36`

**Evidence.** programs/slipstream/src/error.rs:5-69 = 59 variants, last three CreditCeilingExceeded (0x138), FillSequenceOutOfRange (0x139), LegacyLayoutRejected (0x13a). frontend/src/lib/slipstream/errors.ts:24-37 = 56 names (ends PositionStillOpen); errors.ts:88 falls through to `custom 0x${code}` and :112 prints 'Rejected on chain: custom 0x138.' with no ERR_ADVICE. keepers/src/shared/ertx.ts:21-33 = 56 names (same gap). tests/integration/crank_twap_harness.ts:49-61 and tests/integration/liquidation.test.ts:120-132 = 53 names (also missing TriggerConditionNotMet, SelfTrade, PositionStillOpen). client/src has no errors module at all (client/src/index.ts:1-4). Emitters: withdraw_trading_credit.rs:64 (CreditCeilingExceeded), close_trading_credit.rs:76 (LegacyLayoutRejected), settle_trades.rs:157 / settle_from_log.rs:154 (FillSequenceOutOfRange). Concrete browser path: frontend/src/hooks/use-session.ts:1123-1137 withdraws the FULL `decodeTradingCredit(...).credit` rather than min(credit, creditOutstanding), so any credit whose ledger is below its balance (all 46 pre-deploy credits, ledger measured 0 on chain) hits 0x138.

**Failure.** After deploy, a trader pressing Withdraw on a pre-upgrade credit sees 'Rejected on chain: custom 0x138.' with no advice and no indication that the amount, not the chain, is the problem; keepers log 'unknown(0x138)'/'unknown(0x139)'; the two integration suites cannot name any error added since Round 6.

**Fix.** Move errors.ts into client/src (export ERR_NAMES from one generated file: a ~10-line prebuild that parses the enum body of programs/slipstream/src/error.rs), append the three names now, import it from keepers/src/shared/ertx.ts and both integration tests, and re-vendor to the frontend. Add ERR_ADVICE for CreditCeilingExceeded. In use-session.ts:1124-1128 withdraw min(credit, userAccount.creditOutstanding).


### [medium/both] client/src decodeTradingCredit claims to accept the legacy 56-byte layout but reads bytes 56..96 unconditionally and throws RangeError on every live legacy credit; the frontend twin is the correct copy

`client/src/accounts.ts:271`  · prior: S1-01

**Evidence.** client/src/accounts.ts:254-256 relaxes the length check to 56 ('Accept the legacy 56-byte layout ... See the frontend twin for the full note') but :270-271 still do readPubkey(data,56) and readI64LE(data,88). Verified: Buffer.alloc(56).readBigInt64LE(88) -> RangeError ERR_OUT_OF_RANGE. frontend/src/lib/slipstream/accounts.ts:319-345 has the real fix (isLegacy gate, TRADING_CREDIT_LEGACY_SIZE). Live L1 count of 56-byte DISC_TRADING_CREDIT accounts: 4 program-owned (13,313.24 USDC, the S1-01 accounts) + 12 delegation-owned (36,470.00 USDC); ER shows 16 of 37 credits at 56 bytes. Consumers on the throwing copy: keepers/src/shared/bot-wallets.ts:270-273 swallows the throw and returns null; keepers/src/topup-takers.ts:84-88 then treats null as 'no active orders / nothing committed' and proceeds to undelegate (the guard it prints is 'undelegation would orphan ER slots'); keepers/src/inspect-user.ts:41 and verify-session.ts:197 throw outright.

**Failure.** Any keeper or script built on client/src cannot read a legacy credit at all: the market-maker/taker bots report 'no trading credit found' for a wallet that holds funds, and topup-takers skips its orphaned-slot guard exactly for the accounts that most need it. The fix that closed this in the frontend never reached the declared source of truth.

**Fix.** Port frontend accounts.ts:319-345 verbatim into client/src/accounts.ts:253-274 (TRADING_CREDIT_LEGACY_SIZE, isLegacy, ZERO_PUBKEY/0n fallback) and re-vendor. No program change.


### [low/both] Divergence is now bidirectional, so neither copy can be declared the winner as-is: the frontend holds three correctness fixes the client lacks, the client holds the FillLog surface the frontend lacks, and the header comment cites a copy script that does not copy the SDK

`frontend/src/lib/slipstream/constants.ts:7`  · prior: S6-04

**Evidence.** diff client/src vs frontend/src/lib/slipstream: accounts.ts +58/-47 lines, instructions.ts +18/-190, constants.ts +19/-12, pda.ts +10/-19. Frontend-only and correct: isMarkPriceFresh/markPriceAgeMins/MARK_PRICE_MAX_STALENESS_MINS (accounts.ts:179-236, mirrors market.rs:114,252-259), legacy TradingCredit decode (:319-345), decodeOrderBook truncation guard (:568-587, mirrors order_book.rs:92-100), SENTINEL (constants.ts:118, S6-04), explicit `import { Buffer } from "buffer"` (constants.ts:15, pda.ts:10, instructions.ts:18; the 'writeBigInt64LE is not a function' note). Client-only and correct: SEED_FILL_LOG, IX_INITIALIZE_FILL_LOG..IX_SETTLE_FROM_LOG, findFillLogPda, decodeFillLogHeader/decodeFillLogFills, five FillLog builders (client/src/instructions.ts:1272-1452), FILL_LOG_HEADER_SIZE/CAPACITY. Every SHARED decoder offset and builder account order was checked function-by-function against state/*.rs #[repr(C)] and each handler's `let [..] = accounts` destructure (37 builders, 12 layouts): all identical and all correct in both copies. frontend/src/lib/slipstream/constants.ts:7 says the copy is made by 'scripts/copy-manifest.mjs'; frontend/scripts/copy-manifest.mjs:1-40 copies only deploy.json. frontend/next.config.ts is empty (no turbopack.root / externalDir), so the outside-project-root constraint that motivated vendoring is real.

**Failure.** Every fix lands in one copy only (three did on 2026-08-22/23 per git log of accounts.ts), and 'keep in sync' is a comment nobody runs. The next layout or account-order change will ship to the browser or to the keepers, not both.

**Fix.** Single source of truth = client/src, after porting the three frontend fixes + SENTINEL into it and prepending `import { Buffer } from "buffer"` to its four files (harmless under node). Then add ~6 lines to the EXISTING prebuild hook frontend/scripts/copy-manifest.mjs (already wired to predev/prebuild in frontend/package.json:8,10): copy client/src/{accounts,constants,instructions,pda,errors}.ts into frontend/src/lib/slipstream/, gitignore those five, keep orderbook.ts and index.ts frontend-authored. Skipped: a monorepo package/tsconfig-paths route, add when Next's outside-root bundling is configured.


### [low/both] The program's mark-freshness gate is mirrored only in the frontend copy; the trigger-executing keeper on the client copy sends execute_trigger against an unchecked last_mark_price

`keepers/src/liquidation-keeper.ts:323`  · prior: S6-X02

**Evidence.** isMarkPriceFresh exists only at frontend/src/lib/slipstream/accounts.ts:196-204; client/src/accounts.ts has no equivalent (grep markPriceMinute/isMarkPriceFresh in keepers/src: none). keepers/src/liquidation-keeper.ts:321-329 picks closePrice = market.lastMarkPrice > 0 ? lastMarkPrice : oracle and calls executeTriggers; execute_trigger.rs:82-83 uses mark_price_for_close(now) and returns OracleStale when the stamp is >30 min old (market.rs:252-259). Live: markPriceMinute=56407 vs now_min=56410, age 3 min, so it does not bind today; with the Pyth devnet feed publishing ~every 309s against a 60s gate (established 2026-09-04) the crank can stall and the stamp age crosses 30.

**Failure.** During a crank stall every triggered SL/TP is sent, reverts OracleStale, is retried each poll, and the keeper's own log reports the trigger as executable. No funds move; fees and log noise only.

**Fix.** Port isMarkPriceFresh/markPriceAgeMins/MARK_PRICE_MAX_STALENESS_MINS into client/src/accounts.ts (part of the SSOT port) and gate executeTriggers on it in liquidation-keeper.ts:321.


### [low/both] decodeOrderBook in the client copy has no truncation guard; a short buffer yields RangeError or a silent short-slice PublicKey

`client/src/accounts.ts:497`

**Evidence.** client/src/accounts.ts:497-527 walks maxOrderSlots/maxPriceLevelsPerSide/maxFillEvents from the header with no length check; readPubkey (:49-51) uses Buffer.subarray which returns a short view silently. frontend/src/lib/slipstream/accounts.ts:568-587 adds the computeOrderBookAccountSize check that mirrors order_book.rs:92-100. Keeper readers (fill-log-keeper.ts, taker-bot.ts bestBid/bestAsk :79-98) read the full ~626 KB account today, so no live consumer passes a dataSlice; the landing page reads a 48-byte header slice and hand-reads offset 40 (settlement-status.tsx:54-61) rather than use the decoder.

**Failure.** A dataSlice, an RPC that truncates, or a header bumped by grow_orderbook ahead of realloc produces either an unnamed RangeError or a garbage owner key in the client-copy consumers; same class as the guard the frontend already needed.

**Fix.** Port frontend accounts.ts:568-587 into client/src/accounts.ts:498 as part of the SSOT port.


### [low/repo] decodeGlobalState in both copies cannot see pending_authority (offset 104) or keeper (offset 136), so no TS consumer can verify propose/accept_authority or set_keeper took effect

`client/src/accounts.ts:69`

**Evidence.** global_state.rs:10-11 KEEPER_OFFSET=136, KEEPER_EXTENDED_LEN=168; propose_authority.rs:41-42 PENDING_AUTHORITY_OFFSET=104, EXTENDED_LEN=136; both live past GlobalState::LEN=104. client/src/accounts.ts:69-83 and frontend accounts.ts (same body) decode only bytes 0..104. Live GlobalState length = 104 (never extended), authority A5sV4Pkk..., market_count 1, paused 0.

**Failure.** After set_keeper runs, keepers/inspect scripts still report only `authority`; an operator cannot confirm which key the FillLog instructions will accept, and a pending authority rotation is invisible to every dashboard.

**Fix.** Add `pendingAuthority: data.length >= 136 ? readPubkey(data,104) : null` and `keeper: data.length >= 168 ? readPubkey(data,136) : null` to decodeGlobalState (guarded like the Market Round-3 tail), in client/src then re-vendor.


### [low/repo] frontend imports `buffer` explicitly in four SDK files but does not declare it; it resolves only through @solana/web3.js's transitive dependency

`frontend/package.json:21`

**Evidence.** frontend/src/lib/slipstream/{constants.ts:15,pda.ts:10,instructions.ts:18,accounts.ts} do `import { Buffer } from "buffer"` with the note that the ambient polyfill lacks writeBigInt64LE. frontend/package.json dependencies (lines 15-40) list @solana/web3.js ^1.98.4 and @solana/spl-token but no `buffer`.

**Failure.** A web3.js major bump or a package-manager hoisting change removes the transitive `buffer` and the browser build regresses to 'data.writeBigInt64LE is not a function' on every instruction builder.

**Fix.** Add "buffer": "^6.0.3" to frontend/package.json dependencies.



## test-integrity  (16 findings)

**Measured:** Suite on 87f9eb1 (SBF .so built 2026-09-05 01:51 IST, after HEAD): `cargo test --locked` in tests/unit = 123 passed / 0 failed (0.15 s parallel, 0.90 s with --test-threads=1); 68 process_instruction calls; 23 of 43 discriminators driven, 20 uncovered (prior repro script reports 27 because its regex misses `const IX_*` style); 22 of 27 test files build a Mollusk harness, 3 pin the clock, 22 call set_var; 11 exact-Custom assertions vs 18 bare !Success assertions; 0 Custom() assertions in the 7 R-round files; 12 tautological post-rejection state assertions across 8 files (Mollusk 0.12 returns `accounts.to_vec()` on failure, lib.rs:1122-1126). Same-instruction replays feeding resulting_accounts: 0 (7 tests make 2 calls, all against the base fixture). Program error enum: 59 variants (0x100..0x13a); frontend and keeper ERR_NAMES tables: 56 each. Live devnet reads 2026-09-05 unix 1788585604: program-owned accounts 100 (GlobalState 1 @104 B, Market 1 @2064 B, UserAccount 52 @56 B, Position 41 @96 B, TradingCredit 4 @56 B + 1 @96 B); UserAccount free_collateral total 551,264.962526 USDC, 0 of 52 with reserved_margin>0, 13 of 52 with pending_fills>0 holding 438,643.100422 USDC; program-owned credits: 3x50 USDC + 13,163.24 legacy + 500 modern; delegation-program-owned byte0==6 accounts: 52 @96 B + 12 @56 B, of which 42 are slipstream credit PDAs (30 modern + 12 legacy, sane credit sum 135,181.60 USDC, 0 inflated) and 22 are foreign (all 22 carry 7_017_392_213_830_636_841 / committed 11_016_084_772_046_666_000, decode as bump 152, market_index 25900); vault BTWVG5… = 725,915.845954 USDC; Pyth J83w4 (integration pin) publish 1724967841 = 736.3 days stale; Pyth 7UVim (deploy.json) age 217 s at read; GlobalState live length 104 (unextended; matches the R7 fallback fixture).

**Not covered:** Could not execute the integration suite (needs devnet signer/operator key and a live ER) — assessed statically only. Did not enumerate the delegation program's ER-side state (devnet.magicblock.app) for the 42 credits; L1 owner/PDA attribution only. Did not read test_state.rs/test_order_book.rs line-by-line beyond the two protected tests and the assertion inventory (pure-struct tests, no SVM). Did not attempt to load SPL Token into Mollusk to prove the deposit/withdraw success path is reachable (read-only mandate; crate name and version asserted from the mollusk-svm 0.12 workspace, not compiled). Did not quantify keeper-side pending_fills symmetry (lives in keepers/, outside tests/).


### [medium/repo] Mollusk returns the INPUT accounts on failure, so every 'state untouched after rejection' assertion in the suite is tautological (12 assertions, 8 files)

`tests/unit/src/test_security_regressions.rs:150`  · prior: S11-07

**Evidence.** mollusk-svm-0.12.0/src/lib.rs:1122-1126: `let resulting_accounts = if message_result.raw_result.is_ok() { deconstruct_resulting_accounts(..) } else { accounts.to_vec() }`. Every post-rejection state assertion therefore compares the fixture to itself: test_security_regressions.rs:93-94, :150-155, :206-207 ("mark price must not move" — the prior audit's S11-C2 credited this as a strength); test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:115-123 and :277-278; test_pending_fills_regressions.rs:82; test_legacy_credit_recovery.rs:176-178; test_settlement_reader_regressions.rs:216-217 and :243-248; test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:168-176; test_fill_ring_lossless.rs:192-197; test_close_user_account_regressions.rs:103; test_authority_rotation.rs:151. Also tautological by construction: test_cancel_order_expiry_regressions.rs:138 (credit.owner is never written by cancel_order) and test_funding_clamp_regressions.rs:29 (`rate.abs() <= MAX` after funding.rs:54 `.clamp(-MAX, MAX)`).

**Failure.** A regression that rejects for the WRONG reason (e.g. NotEnoughAccountKeys because an account was added) keeps all of these green while the guarded branch is dead; reviewers reading 'the forged account's lamports must not be drained' believe the drain path was exercised when nothing past the first failing check ran.

**Fix.** Drop the post-rejection state assertions or pair each negative test with the exact `ProgramError::Custom(SlipstreamError::X as u32)` it expects (pattern already in test_global_pause.rs:124-131); prefer a success companion that reaches the same branch (as test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:185 does).


### [medium/repo] None of the 12 P0 remediation tests pins the rejection reason: every R-round negative assertion is `!matches!(Success)`

`tests/unit/src/test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:109`  · prior: S11-07

**Evidence.** grep 'Custom(' over the seven R-round files (test_credit_ceiling_regressions, test_legacy_credit_recovery, test_fill_ring_lossless, test_settlement_reader_regressions, test_pending_fills_regressions, test_keeper_role_regressions, test_funding_clamp_regressions) = 0 matches; 18 of the suite's ~29 rejection assertions are bare `!matches!(res.program_result, MolluskResult::Success)` (test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:109-113, :272-276; test_legacy_credit_recovery.rs:169-174; test_settlement_reader_regressions.rs:211-215, :238-242, :300-307; test_pending_fills_regressions.rs:75-79; test_keeper_role_regressions.rs:103-108, :195-200; test_fill_ring_lossless.rs:187-191; plus test_security_regressions.rs:41-46 assert_rejected x3, test_place_order_regressions.rs:189-193, :247-251, test_withdraw_collateral_regressions.rs:245-249, test_record_pending_fill_regressions.rs:78, test_close_user_account_regressions.rs:94, test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:162). Serial run confirms the codes today are the intended ones (0x138 CreditCeilingExceeded, 0x12b CreditStillActive, 0x134 FillMarginExceeded, 0x139 FillSequenceOutOfRange, 0x11c InvalidFillSequence, 0x101 InvalidAuthority) but nothing asserts them. test_initialize_market_regressions.rs:114-121 asserts InvalidInstructionData, which initialize_market.rs emits from three branches (:67 length, :86 zero params, :91 the fee bound under test).

**Failure.** Any reorder or added precondition that fails earlier keeps the P0 regression green while the P0 branch stops executing — the exact failure mode these tests were pre-landed RED to prevent.

**Fix.** Replace each `!matches!` with `assert_eq!(res.program_result, MolluskResult::Failure(ProgramError::Custom(SlipstreamError::<expected> as u32)))`; no program change.


### [medium/repo] A frozen fixture constant that is 10x wrong forced settlement to ship a 1x-notional margin bound instead of the max_leverage bound the issue asked for

`tests/unit/src/test_settlement_reader_regressions.rs:230`

**Evidence.** test_settlement_reader_regressions.rs:180,204,230,261: `let honest = QTY / MAX_LEVERAGE as u64` = 100_000_000/20 = 5_000_000 — a margin derived from base-atom QUANTITY, not notional. compute_notional(QTY, PRICE) = 100_000_000*100_000_000/1e9 = 10_000_000 (math/fixed_point.rs:53-62), so the honest 20x margin is 500_000; the fixture's 'honest' fill posts 10x that. settle_from_log.rs:182-192 (`ponytail:` comment) says so explicitly: 'the frozen fixture's honest fill posts 5_000_000 against a notional of 10_000_000 — 10x its own stated bound — so the tight bound rejects it and reds three frozen tests', and ships `if fill.filled_margin > fill_notional` (1x) at :190; settle_trades.rs:185 mirrors it. test_settle_rejects_filled_margin_above_leverage_bound (:223-249) is named for a leverage bound it does not assert: forged = 5_000_000_000 is 500x notional, so any margin between 500_001 and 10_000_000 (up to 20x the intended bound) passes it.

**Failure.** Protected tests now guard a bound 20x looser than specified; the only absolute bound is the F1 ledger clamp (settle_from_log.rs:258-273), so a hostile ER can front-load a user's entire funded ledger into one fill's Position.collateral rather than 1/max_leverage of it — bounded by what was funded, but the relational check is decorative and the test that claims to pin it cannot detect its removal.

**Fix.** Correct the fixture: `honest = compute_initial_margin(compute_notional(QTY, PRICE), MAX_LEVERAGE)` = 500_000, add a case at honest*2 that must be rejected, then tighten settle_from_log.rs:190 and settle_trades.rs:185 to `compute_initial_margin(fill_notional, market.max_leverage)` (NEEDS-DEPLOY; the test change is a finding against the protected list, not a proposal to weaken it).


### [medium/repo] R6's catch-up cap (MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS) has zero instruction-level coverage: the only compute_funding fixture uses exactly 3 intervals, which is the cap

`tests/unit/src/test_compute_funding_regressions.rs:99`

**Evidence.** test_compute_funding_regressions.rs:99 `last_funding_ts = NOW - 3 * INTERVAL_SECS`; math/funding.rs:23 `MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS: i64 = 3`. compute_funding.rs:49 `intervals = raw_intervals.min(MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS)` and :51-57 (forgive-excess: `next_funding_ts = now` when raw > cap) versus :66-69 (advance by whole intervals) both evaluate to NOW for this fixture, so the assertion at :127-130 'must advance by whole intervals (not reset to now)' cannot distinguish the branches, and the cap is never exercised (raw_intervals == cap). The R6 test test_funding_clamp_regressions.rs:29-48 asserts only the math seam and constants (`MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS <= 8`, a constant product) — it would pass if compute_funding.rs never referenced MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS.

**Failure.** Deleting `.min(MAX_CATCHUP_INTERVALS)` at compute_funding.rs:49 leaves all 123 tests green while restoring the S3-01 live replay (48 intervals x clamped rate = 24% of notional in one permissionless call).

**Fix.** Add two Mollusk cases: last_funding_ts = NOW - 10*INTERVAL (assert index == 3*rate and last_funding_ts == NOW) and NOW - 2.5*INTERVAL (assert index == 2*rate and last_funding_ts == NOW - 0.5*INTERVAL).


### [medium/repo] R7 keeper role is tested on 1 of the 3 instructions it claims to widen; initialize_fill_log (0x1D) and delegate_fill_log (0x1E) have no test at all

`tests/unit/src/test_keeper_role_regressions.rs:3`  · prior: S11-02

**Evidence.** test_keeper_role_regressions.rs:3-6 and :111-113 describe 'the three gated instructions'; the file drives only IX_RECORD_PENDING_FILL (:79, :131) and IX_SET_KEEPER (:182). The other two gates — initialize_fill_log.rs:64 and delegate_fill_log.rs:102 `GlobalState::is_authority_or_keeper` — appear in no test (coverage map: 0x1D, 0x1E uncovered). initialize_fill_log's pre-CPI path is a system-program CreateAccount, which Mollusk 0.12 does load (program.rs:239-243), so the keeper-accept path is testable today.

**Failure.** The operational goal of R7 (fill-log keeper runs without the upgrade/mint authority) fails at epoch rotation if either untested gate regresses to authority-only, with a green suite.

**Fix.** Add a keeper-signed initialize_fill_log success case and a delegate_fill_log wrong-signer/keeper case (the pin check at delegate_fill_log.rs precedes the delegation CPI, so rejection paths are reachable).


### [medium/both] Global pause is asserted on 1 of 7 wired instructions, and the dispatcher comment names three instructions (cancel, liquidate, funding) that are NOT wired

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:182`

**Evidence.** mod.rs:181-182: 'Non-trivial trading instructions (place/cancel/settle/liquidate/funding) call this'. Actual callers (grep ensure_not_globally_paused): place_order.rs:79, fund_trading_credit.rs:38, place_trigger.rs:57, settle_trades.rs:81, deposit_collateral.rs:41, settle_from_log.rs:62, delegate_trading_credit.rs:107 — cancel_order.rs, liquidate_position.rs, compute_funding.rs, close_position.rs, withdraw_collateral.rs, withdraw_trading_credit.rs, execute_trigger.rs, claim_funding.rs, mirror_fills.rs, crank_twap.rs have no call. test_global_pause.rs:118-145 exercises place_order only.

**Failure.** The emergency switch's scope is documented wrong and pinned by nothing: whether liquidations/funding/withdrawals continue during a pause is decided by omission, and any of the six wired-but-untested gates can be dropped silently.

**Fix.** One table-driven Mollusk test that sets paused=1 and asserts GlobalPaused for each of the seven wired discriminators; fix the mod.rs:182 comment to the real list (the policy question of whether liquidate/withdraw should also pause is for the owner, not this finding).


### [medium/repo] The '22 ER-inflated live credits' cited by the R1 test fixture, seed_credit_ledger's doc block and remediate.md are not slipstream accounts — PDA re-derivation attributes all 22 to a foreign program

`tests/unit/src/test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:231`

**Evidence.** test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:231 `ER_CLAIM = 7_017_392_213_830_636_841; // a real live devnet value`; seed_credit_ledger.rs:21-23 '22 of the 45 live delegated credits currently carry 7_017_392_213_830_636_841'; docs/spec/remediate.md:329-334 (S5-01 'not hypothetical'). Live read 2026-09-05 (unix 1788585604): getProgramAccounts(DELeGG…, dataSize 96, byte0==6) = 52 accounts; re-deriving [b"credit", data[8..40], data[2..4]] under 7qujfsb4… matches exactly 30 of them (+12 at dataSize 56, all matching). The 22 non-matching accounts are the 22 carrying 7_017_392_213_830_636_841: decoded as TradingCredit they read bump=152, market_index=25900, active_orders=59767, committed=11_016_084_772_046_666_000, session_expiry=5 — raw hex 06982c6577e9720f… is an 8-byte Anchor-style discriminator followed by three u64 = 5, i.e. another program's delegated account whose first byte happens to be 0x06. Live slipstream delegated credits: 42 (30 modern + 12 legacy), 0 inflated, sane credit sum 135,181.60 USDC.

**Failure.** The P0 regression test claims to replay a live attack that never occurred, and the program's own source documents an ER corruption of user credits that does not exist; risk prioritisation for the still-undeployed R1 (and the S5-01 narrative) rests on a mis-filtered gPA. R1 remains correct as a threat model, but the 'operator must seed 46 accounts, 22 of which carry impossible values' urgency is wrong.

**Fix.** Change the fixture comment to 'an arbitrary hostile value' and correct seed_credit_ledger.rs:21-23 / remediate.md:329-334; any future live census of delegated credits must filter by re-derived PDA, not by discriminator byte alone.


### [medium/repo] Untested-instruction list on this branch: 20 of 43 discriminators have zero in-SVM coverage (prior script still reports 27 because it cannot resolve named consts)

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/mod.rs:110`  · prior: S11-02

**Evidence.** Coverage map resolving both `vec![0xNN]` and `const IX_X: u8 = 0xNN; vec![IX_X]`: declared 43, driven 23, uncovered 20, 68 process_instruction calls, 123 tests. Ranked by money-path proximity: (1) 0x02 deposit_collateral — vault entry, SPL CPI at the end but vault/mint/pause checks before it (deposit_collateral.rs:41) are reachable; (2) 0x07 claim_funding — moves funding into free_collateral, no CPI, S3-05 P1 open; (3) 0x0D initialize_trading_credit — S1-01's row, system CPI only (loadable); (4) 0x12 undelegate_trading_credit — reconcile_credit money path before the magic CPI; (5) 0x0F delegate_trading_credit — pause+pin checks before CPI (delegate_trading_credit.rs:107-113); (6) 0x25 set_market_oracle — repoints the settlement price source, no CPI; (7) 0x22/0x23 place_trigger/cancel_trigger — rent-bearing PDAs, pause gate at place_trigger.rs:57; (8) 0x1D initialize_fill_log and 0x1E delegate_fill_log — R7 gates; (9) 0x01 initialize_user, 0x19 initialize_position, 0x0C initialize_global — system CPI only; (10) 0x09/0x0A/0x18/0x1A/0x20/0x16/0x17 — delegation lifecycle + grow_orderbook, behind the S11-04 ceiling. Now covered since the prior audit (via R-round files): 0x0E, 0x13, 0x1C, 0x21, 0x28, 0x29, 0x2A. docs/audit/audit-e2e/repro/s11/coverage_map.py:29 regex `data(?:\s*[:=]\s*)vec!\[\s*0x` misses the R-round style and prints 27 uncovered.

**Failure.** The seven instructions that are testable with the default Mollusk builtins (0x07, 0x25, 0x0D, 0x01, 0x19, 0x0C, 0x1D) sit on user money or the price source and have no automated evidence in either direction.

**Fix.** Add one Mollusk test per row in the ranked order above, starting with claim_funding, set_market_oracle (stranger rejected, authority repoints) and deposit_collateral's pre-CPI rejections; update the repro script regex to resolve `const IX_*` definitions.


### [medium/repo] Still zero same-instruction replay tests: 7 tests now issue two calls but all run against the base fixture, never against resulting_accounts

`tests/unit/src/test_settlement_reader_regressions.rs:174`  · prior: S11-01

**Evidence.** replay_probe.py (patched to resolve named consts): 7 tests issue 2 calls — test_authority_rotation.rs (0x26→0x27), test_credit_ceiling_regressions.rs:169/206 (0x0E→0x13, the only one that feeds resulting state forward, into a DIFFERENT instruction), :271/282 (0x28 stranger then authority on `base`), test_keeper_role_regressions.rs:92/103 and :195/202, test_pending_fills_regressions.rs:74/85 — each second call reuses the untouched `accounts` vector. settle_from_log's exactly-once (settle_from_log.rs:160-162, :356-358 FillQueueEmpty when nothing new) and mirror_fills' cursor idempotence are executed once and discarded; the oracle hysteresis (oracle.rs:339-350, AGREEMENT_HYSTERESIS_COUNT=3) needs three sequential compute_funding calls and has none.

**Failure.** Restates S11-01: double-settle, re-mirror and restricted-mode-never-clears defects are invisible to a suite where no instruction ever sees the state it left behind.

**Fix.** For settle_from_log (0x21), mirror_fills (0x1F) and compute_funding (0x06): call once, pass `res.resulting_accounts` as the account set, call again, assert FillQueueEmpty / unchanged cursor / restricted_mode clears on the 3rd agreement.


### [medium/repo] withdraw_collateral's three value gates (pending_fills, reserved_margin, insufficient free) — including the gate R1 turned live — have no test; the success path remains structurally unreachable

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/withdraw_collateral.rs:85`  · prior: S11-04

**Evidence.** withdraw_collateral.rs:78-91 Gate 1 PendingFillsExist, Gate 2 `if user.reserved_margin > 0 → ReservedMarginExists` ('Dead until the credit ledger went live; now it means withdraw your credit first'), Gate 3 InsufficientCollateral. tests/unit/src/test_withdraw_collateral_regressions.rs has three tests (:153 vault pin, :190 same-slot, :227 omitted position) — none sets pending_fills, reserved_margin or an over-large amount. Live: 13 of 52 UserAccounts have pending_fills>0 holding 438,643.10 of 551,264.96 USDC free_collateral (Gate 1 binds today); 0 of 52 have reserved_margin>0 (Gate 2 is dead on the deployed binary and becomes live for every funded user on the R1 deploy). The SPL Token transfer at :140-146 is unreachable because tests/unit/Cargo.toml:12 loads only mollusk-svm 0.12 with default builtins (system + loaders) and no `mollusk-svm-programs-token`.

**Failure.** After the R1 deploy every user with a funded credit is refused ALL withdrawals of their free collateral until they withdraw the credit first — a user-visible funds-locked behaviour with no test describing it — and no test has ever moved a token out of the vault.

**Fix.** Add `mollusk-svm-programs-token = "0.12"` and load `mollusk_svm_programs_token::token::add_program(&mut m)`; then one round-trip test deposit→withdraw asserting vault/user token balances, plus three one-line negative cases for Gates 1-3 with exact error codes.


### [medium/repo] The ER trust boundary is still asserted by no test: 9 delegation-lifecycle instructions untested and 13 hardcoded program-id literals compared by nothing

`programs/slipstream/src/instructions/delegate_trading_credit.rs:112`  · prior: S11-03

**Evidence.** er_boundary_probe.py on 87f9eb1: 0x09, 0x0A, 0x0F, 0x12, 0x16, 0x18, 0x1A, 0x1E, 0x20 'no test'; DELEGATION_PROGRAM_ID 3 copies, MAGIC_PROGRAM_ID 5, MAGIC_CONTEXT_ID 5, 'test assertions over any of these constants: 0'. The pin check `if *delegation_program.key() != DELEGATION_PROGRAM_ID { InvalidProgramId }` at delegate_trading_credit.rs:112-113 runs before any CPI (first invoke_signed at :207), so the rejection is reachable in the default harness.

**Failure.** Restates S11-03: a one-byte edit to one of the 13 literals repoints a delegation or commit CPI at a decoy program with 123 green tests.

**Fix.** One pure-Rust test asserting the 13 literals are byte-equal to a single `pub(crate)` canonical constant, and one Mollusk test per delegate_* passing a wrong delegation-program account and asserting Custom(InvalidProgramId=0x129).


### [low/repo] Both TypeScript error tables stop at 56 entries while the program enum has 59; nothing tests parity and the three R-round codes decode as unknown

`frontend/src/lib/slipstream/errors.ts:23`

**Evidence.** frontend/src/lib/slipstream/errors.ts:23-36 ERR_NAMES = 56 names ending PositionStillOpen; keepers/src/shared/ertx.ts:21-49 ERR_NAMES = 56, `unknown(0x…)` past the end; programs/slipstream/src/error.rs:66-69 appends CreditCeilingExceeded (0x138), FillSequenceOutOfRange (0x139), LegacyLayoutRejected (0x13a) 'keepers decode by ordinal'. No test in tests/unit, frontend, keepers or client compares the tables to error.rs (frontend/keepers/client package.json have no test script; zero *.test.ts under their src/).

**Failure.** On the R-round deploy, a trader hitting the credit ceiling and a keeper hitting FillSequenceOutOfRange both see 'unknown(0x138)' / a raw code; the S8-C7 data-honesty contract has no automated guard for the one table that turns codes into words.

**Fix.** Append the three names to both tables and add a 10-line parity check (generate the list from error.rs in CI, or assert length == 59 and the last name).


### [low/repo] Weak or misnamed assertions on the two tests that exercise the real matching/settlement money path

`tests/unit/src/test_place_order_regressions.rs:309`  · prior: S11-07

**Evidence.** test_place_order_regressions.rs:307-312: the only test that drives a real crossing fill through the compiled program asserts `credit.credit < 10 * PRICE_SCALE` — 'debited for the real fill' — without pinning the amount (expected 10 USDC − 0.75 margin − 0.015 taker fee = 9.235 USDC; any debit of 1 atom passes). test_fill_ring_lossless.rs:78 `test_place_order_refuses_to_overwrite_unmirrored_fill` never invokes place_order: it calls OrderBookView::push_fill_event directly (:90); the instruction-level behaviour (place_order.rs:458 `ob.push_fill_event(fill)?` halting a crossing order with FillQueueFull) is untested. test_cancel_order_expiry_regressions.rs:106 pins the permissionless path only with the owner's credit; the NotOrderOwner branch at cancel_order.rs:85-87 (a canceller passing THEIR OWN credit to capture the freed margin) is exercised by no test — the S11-C3 'correctly-typed account of a different owner' class is still empty suite-wide.

**Failure.** A margin/fee arithmetic regression in place_order, or a regression that lets a stranger's credit absorb an expired order's margin, is undetectable by the suite.

**Fix.** Assert the exact post-fill credit (9_235_000); add a place_order case against a full ring asserting FillQueueFull and an untouched book; add a cancel_order case passing the canceller's own credit and asserting NotOrderOwner (0x114).

### [low/repo] Only 3 of 22 Mollusk files pin the harness clock; the R-round P0 tests all inherit unix_timestamp=0 / slot=0 implicitly

`tests/unit/src/test_keeper_role_regressions.rs:26`  · prior: S11-07

**Evidence.** grep -l sysvars.clock tests/unit/src → test_cancel_order_expiry_regressions.rs, test_compute_funding_regressions.rs, test_oracle_restricted_mode_regressions.rs only. test_liquidate_position_regressions.rs:45-47 and test_security_regressions.rs:117-122 rely on publish_time=0 == Clock::default() in a comment; test_place_order_regressions.rs:341-344 and :394-395 compute staleness from an assumed now_min=0; test_withdraw_collateral_regressions.rs:194-195 assumes slot 0. None asserts the assumption.

**Failure.** A Mollusk default-clock change silently flips freshness/same-slot outcomes across 19 files, and a test that passes for the wrong reason pins nothing (the suite's own words at test_security_regressions.rs:119).

**Fix.** Set `m.sysvars.clock.unix_timestamp`/`slot` explicitly in every harness helper.
